   683	fn respond_with_sync(request: Request, status: u16, body: impl latency::Reply, allow_origin: bool, sync_overflow: Option<Json>, shared: Option<&Shared>) {
   684	    respond_full(request, status, body, allow_origin, sync_overflow, shared, Vec::new())
   685	}
   686	
   687	/// `respond_timed` with extra headers (for example Retry-After).
   688	pub(crate) fn respond_with_headers(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, body: impl latency::Reply, headers: Vec<Header>) {
   689	    respond_full(request, status, body, true, None, Some(shared), headers)
   690	}
   691	
   692	fn respond_full(request: Request, status: u16, body: impl latency::Reply, allow_origin: bool, sync_overflow: Option<Json>, shared: Option<&Shared>, extra: Vec<Header>) {
   693	    let refused = status >= 400 || body.refused();
   694	    trace::mark("encode_start");
   695	    let mut bytes = JsonBuffer(Vec::new());
   696	    let generation = if status == 401 { None } else { body.generation().or_else(crate::release_requests::reply_generation).or_else(|| shared.map(|s| *lock(&s.generation))) };
   697	    crate::release_requests::executed();
   698	    let encoded = serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &body);
   699	    // Append only to envelopes without a generation, without cloning row payloads.
   700	    let stamped = if encoded.is_ok() && body.generation().is_none() && let Some(generation)=generation && bytes.0.last()==Some(&b'}') {
   701	        bytes.0.pop();
   702	        let separator=if bytes.0.len()>1 {","} else {""};
   703	        write!(&mut bytes,"{separator}\"generation\":{generation}}}")
   704	    } else { Ok(()) };
   705	    drop(body);
   706	    let status = if encoded.is_err() || stamped.is_err() {
   707	        bytes.0.clear();
   708	        let refusal = Refusal::new("E_BOUND", "bound", "the HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB; request a smaller page or return fewer values").guide("reads");
   709	        // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.11 — generation discovery survives an undeliverable row page.
   710	        let mut reply = sync_overflow.unwrap_or_else(|| json!({}));
   711	        reply["denied"] = refusal_json(&refusal);
   712	        if let Some(generation)=generation {reply["generation"]=json!(generation);}
   713	        serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &reply).expect("the fixed HTTP refusal and sync identity fit the response ceiling");
   714	        200
   715	    } else { status };
   716	    let refused = refused || encoded.is_err();
   717	    trace::mark("encode_end");
   718	    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::response(&bytes.0);
   719	    let capacity = bytes.0.len() + 512;
   720	    let mut response = Response::from_data(bytes.0).with_status_code(status);
   721	    if let Some(shared) = shared {
   722	        for header in clock_headers(shared).into_iter().chain(ephemeral_headers(shared)) { response.add_header(header); }
   723	        // @ref LLP 3000.023#11 — discover compiler dependency summaries without probing older servers.
   724	        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Settlement", "1").unwrap());
   725	        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Settlement, X-Snapback-Generation").unwrap());
   726	    }
   727	    if let Some(generation)=generation {response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Generation",generation.to_string()).unwrap());}
   728	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "application/json").unwrap());
   729	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "no-store").unwrap());
   730	    for header in extra { response.add_header(header); }
   731	    if allow_origin {
   732	        for header in cors(&request) { response.add_header(header); }
   733	    }
   734	    if let Some(header) = latency::header() { response.add_header(header); }
   735	    trace::mark("write_start");
   736	    if status >= 400 && request.method() == &Method::Post {
   737	        let _ = request.respond_and_close_with_completion(response, |result| latency::finished(result.is_err() || refused));
   738	        return;
   739	    }
   740	    // `into_writer` drops the request reader before it returns. tiny_http
   741	    // prebuffers ordinary bodies up to 1 KiB; other readers may drain on
   742	    // drop, so keep its response-before-drain order for those requests.
   743	    let may_drain = request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > 1024) || request.headers().iter().any(|h| {
   744	        h.field.equiv("Expect") || h.field.equiv("Transfer-Encoding")
   745	            || (h.field.equiv("Connection") && h.value.as_str().split(',').any(|v| v.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("upgrade")))
   746	    });
   747	    if may_drain {
   748	        let _ = request.respond_checked_with_completion(response, |result| latency::finished(result.is_err() || refused));
   749	        return;
   750	    }
   751	    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — coalesce buffered JSON HTTP responses
   752	    // The body is already materialized. Frame it before writing so the HTTP
   753	    // library's small socket buffer cannot split headers from a short body
   754	    // and leave the latter waiting for a delayed TCP acknowledgement.
   755	    let mut encoded = Vec::with_capacity(capacity);
   756	    let response = request.prepare_response(response);
   757	    if response.raw_print(&mut encoded, request.http_version().clone(), request.headers(), request.method() == &Method::Head, None).is_ok() {
   758	        let mut writer = request.into_writer();
   759	        trace::mark("write_start");
   760	        latency::finished(writer.write_all(&encoded).and_then(|_| writer.flush()).is_err() || refused);
   761	    }
   762	}
   763	
   764	/// A 204 has no representation metadata or advertised bytes. Keeping it out
   765	/// of the JSON response helper also prevents an unread `null` from framing the
   766	/// next response on a persistent connection.
   767	pub(crate) fn respond_empty(request: Request, status: u16) {
   768	    let mut response = Response::empty(status);
   769	    if let Some(header)=generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
   770	    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()).filter(|header| !header.field.equiv("Content-Type")) {
   771	        response.add_header(header);
   772	    }
   773	    let _ = request.respond(response);
   774	}
   775	
   776	pub(crate) fn refusal_json(refusal: &Refusal) -> Json {
   777	    serde_json::to_value(refusal).unwrap_or(Json::Null)
   778	}
   779	
   780	
   781	pub(crate) fn query_params(url: &str) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
   782	    fn decode(text: &str) -> String {
   783	        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(text.len());
   784	        let bytes = text.as_bytes();
   785	        let mut at = 0;
   786	        while at < bytes.len() {
   787	            if bytes[at] == b'%' && at + 2 < bytes.len() {
   788	                let hex = |b: u8| (b as char).to_digit(16).map(|n| n as u8);
   789	                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (hex(bytes[at+1]), hex(bytes[at+2])) { out.push(a * 16 + b); at += 3; continue; }
   790	            }
   791	            out.push(if bytes[at] == b'+' { b' ' } else { bytes[at] }); at += 1;
   792	        }
   793	        String::from_utf8(out).unwrap_or_else(|_| text.to_owned())
   794	    }
   795	    url.split_once('?')
   796	        .map(|(_, query)| query.split('&').filter_map(|pair| pair.split_once('=').map(|(k, v)| (decode(k), decode(v)))).collect())
   797	        .unwrap_or_default()
   798	}
   799	
   800	// Identity and head are a single snapshot from the owner of the evaluated
   801	// operation. Mutation replay's seq is historical; head is never that receipt.
   802	fn response_head(engine: &mut Engine) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
   803	    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
   804	    let id: String = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::from)?;
   805	    Ok((id, tx.next_seq()? - 1))
   806	}
   807	
   808	// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a replay belongs to its acknowledged store, even if
   809	// the endpoint changes between the client's probe and this transaction.
   810	fn intent_store(engine: &Engine, payload: &Json) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
   811	    let Some(expected) = payload.get("store_id").filter(|id| !id.is_null()) else { return Ok(()); };
   812	    let current = engine.store().meta("store_id")?;
   813	    if expected.as_str().is_some_and(|id| Some(id) == current.as_deref()) { return Ok(()); }
   814	    Err(Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the intent belongs to another store; its original outcome is unknown")
   815	        .site("request.store_id").rewrite("reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent").guide("sync"))
   816	}
   817	
   818	fn operation_refusal(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, why: Refusal) {
   819	    let metadata = shared.read_identity();
   820	    match metadata {
   821	        Ok((store_id, head, generation)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why), "store_id": store_id, "head": head, "generation": generation })),
   822	        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why) })),
   823	    }
   824	}
   825	
   826	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a racing id conflict is a terminal input refusal, not a transport failure.
   827	fn mutation_error_status(refusal: &Refusal) -> u16 {
   828	    if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if matches!(refusal.code.as_str(), "E_ID_REUSED" | "E_DELIVERY_BINDING") { 200 } else { 500 }
   829	}
   830	
   831	fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
   832	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — lookup, decode and execution share the admitted release.
   833	    let operation = request.url().split('?').next().is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("/q/") || p.starts_with("/m/"));
   834	    let _scope=crate::release_requests::Scope::begin(*lock(&shared.generation));
   835	
   836	    request.set_cleanup_guard(latency::FinishOnDrop);
   837	    #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.request_work);
   838	    let method = request.method().clone();
   839	    let url = request.url().to_owned();
   840	    let path = url.split('?').next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
   841	    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::begin(shared, &path);
   842	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — closed read allow-list precedes every handler,
   843	    // declared route, dev door and idempotency replay. Refusals carry no head.
   844	    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
   845	    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
   846	    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.
   847	    let public_door = path.starts_with("/dev/") || matches!(path.as_str(), "/seed" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/login" | "/auth/guest")
   848	        || path == token::ENDPOINT || routes::declared(shared, &url).is_some();
   849	    if request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("authorization")
   850	        && (public_door || crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str()).is_none_or(|token| token.starts_with("ro.")))) {
   851	        match principal_of(&request, shared) {
   852	            Ok((_, _, snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read)) if !read_permitted(&method, &path) =>
   853	                return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_READ_ONLY", "auth", "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes").guide("read-only")})),
   854	            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — the private-proof door keeps one indistinguishable refusal.
   855	            Err(_) if path == token::ENDPOINT => return token::denied(request),
   856	            Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable {503} else {401}, json!({"denied": refusal_json(&why)})),
   857	            _ => {}
   858	        }
   859	    }
   860	    if path == token::ENDPOINT { return token::handle(request, shared); }
   861	    // This exact declared POST path is the only non-loopback development
   862	    // exception. Core doors, mismatched methods and query variants stay fenced.
   863	    if method == Method::Post && let Some((name, route)) = routes::declared(shared, &url) {
   864	        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
   865	    }
   866	    let ephemeral_request = method == Method::Post && path.strip_prefix("/m/").is_some_and(|op| lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations.get(op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral));
   867	    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
   868	        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
   869	        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
   870	        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
   871	    };
   872	    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
   873	        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
   874	        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
   875	        return;
   876	    }
   877	    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
   878	    if shared.personas && (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
   879	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&dev_doors::closed())}));
   880	    }
   881	    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas {
   882	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
   883	    }
   884	    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
   885	    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
   886	    if method==Method::Post && matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") {
   887	        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
   888	            Ok(None)=>{},
   889	            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
   890	            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),
   891	        }
   892	    }
   893	    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
   894	        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
   895	    }
   896	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
   897	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {
   898	        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "fixture catalog requires dev"))})); }
   899	        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
   900	        let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();
   901	        drop(bound);
   902	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "tables": tables }));
   903	    }
   904	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
   905	        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
   906	        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.
   907	        let generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
   908	        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
   909	        let status = lock(&shared.adoption);
   910	        let now = shared.now();
   911	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
   912	    }
   913	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
   914	    if method == Method::Options {
   915	        respond_empty(request, 204);
   916	        return;
   917	    }
   918	    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
   919	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
   920	    }
   921	    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
   922	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
   923	    }
   924	    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
   925	    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow
   926	    // upload cannot retain the authority held at its start.
   927	    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside
   928	    // the SQLite writer transaction that closes the live session family.
   929	    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")
   930	        || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && path.ends_with("/cancel"))
   931	        || matches!(path.as_str(), "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout-all")) {
   932	        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
   933	            // No authenticated viewer: metadata cannot disclose a store head.
   934	            if path.starts_with("/q/") { return respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
   935	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   936	        }
   937	    }
   938	    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/auth/revoke" || path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && !path.ends_with("/cancel"))) {
   939	        if let Err(refusal) = service_of(&request, shared) {
   940	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   941	        }
   942	    }
   943	    if method == Method::Get && matches!(path.as_str(), "/" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/changes") {
   944	        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
   945	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   946	        }
   947	    }
   948	    let _upload_permit = if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
   949	        match crate::assets::admit(shared) {
   950	            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
   951	            None => {
   952	                let mut refusal = Refusal::new("E_RATE_LIMIT", "auth", "too many concurrent uploads; retry shortly");
   953	                refusal.retryable = true;
   954	                return respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
   955	            }
   956	        }
   957	    } else { None };
   958	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
   959	    // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file upload streams at most the schema's largest file bound, refused before reading when declared larger.
   960	    let file_upload = method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" && crate::assets::is_file_upload(&url);
   961	    if method == Method::Post {
   962	        let ceiling = match path.as_str() {
   963	            "/assets" if file_upload => match crate::assets::file_ceiling(shared) {
   964	                Ok(ceiling) => ceiling,
   965	                Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 415, refusal),
   966	            },
   967	            "/assets" => crate::assets::UPLOAD_CEILING,
   968	            "/seed" | "/dev/reset" => SEED_REQUEST_CEILING,
   969	            "/sync" => SYNC_REQUEST_CEILING,
   970	            path if path.starts_with("/auth/") => crate::auth::BODY_CEILING,
   971	            path if path.starts_with("/following/") => 64 * 1024,
   972	            _ => JSON_REQUEST_CEILING,
   973	        };
   974	        let length = request.body_length();
   975	        let too_large = || if file_upload {
   976	            Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", "asset", format!("the file exceeds {ceiling} bytes, the largest file column's bound")).guide("assets")
   977	        } else {
   978	            Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body exceeds its {ceiling}-byte limit; split it into smaller requests"))
   979	        };
   980	        if length.is_some_and(|length| length > ceiling) {
   981	            return reject(request, 413, too_large());
   982	        }
   983	        let mut buffer = [0; 8 * 1024];
   984	        loop {
   985	            let length = buffer.len().min(ceiling - bytes.len() + 1);
   986	            match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..length]) {
   987	                Ok(0) => break,
   988	                Ok(read) if read > ceiling - bytes.len() => return reject(request, 413, too_large()),
   989	                Ok(read) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..read]),
   990	                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
   991	                Err(error) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("cannot read the request body: {error}"))),
   992	            }
   993	        }
   994	        if length.is_some_and(|length| length != bytes.len()) {
   995	            return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "the request body ended before its declared Content-Length"));
   996	        }
   997	    }
   998	    if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
   999	        return crate::assets::upload(request, bytes, shared);
  1000	    }
  1001	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id)=path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix("/declare")){return crate::assets::declare(request,id,bytes,shared);}
  1002	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/finish")) {
  1003	        return crate::assets::finish(request, id, bytes, shared);
  1004	    }
  1005	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/cancel")) {
  1006	        return crate::assets::cancel(request, id, shared);
  1007	    }
  1008	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/assets/intents" { return crate::assets::intents(request, shared); }
  1009	    if method == Method::Get && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/") {
  1010	        return crate::assets::serve(request, id, shared);
  1011	    }
  1012	    let body = match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
  1013	        Ok(body) => body,
  1014	        Err(_) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "a JSON request body must be valid UTF-8")),
  1015	    };
  1016	    let request_now = if (method == Method::Get && path == "/changes")
  1017	        || (method == Method::Post && (path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/m/")))
  1018	    {
  1019	        match shared.advance_time_audiences().map_err(|error| error.refusal()) {
  1020	            Ok(now) => Some(now),
  1021	            Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 503, refusal),
  1022	        }
  1023	    } else { None };
  1024	    if operation && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
  1025	    match (method, path.as_str()) {
  1026	        (Method::Post, "/dev/clock/advance") => maintenance::advance(request, shared, &body),
  1027	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/jobs/".len()..], &body),
  1028	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/dev/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/dev/jobs/".len()..], &body),
  1029	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/a/") => actions::call(request, shared, path.trim_start_matches("/a/"), &body),
  1030	        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim") => actions::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1031	        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim-set") => actions::claim_set(request, shared, &body),
  1032	        (Method::Post, "/routes/verify/claim") => routes::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1033	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") => routes::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
  1034	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/actions/") => actions::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
  1035	        (Method::Post, "/effects/claim") => effects::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1036	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/effects/") => effects::post(request, shared, path, &body),
  1037	        (Method::Get, "/effects") => effects::list(request, shared, &url),
  1038	        (Method::Get | Method::Post, "/probe") => match principal_of(&request, shared) {
  1039	            Ok(viewer) => {
  1040	                let identity = shared.read_identity();
  1041	                match identity {
  1042	                    Ok((store_id, seq,generation)) => {
  1043	                        let mut reply = json!({ "ok": true, "store_id": store_id, "seq": seq, "generation":generation,"viewer": viewer.0 });
  1044	                        if query_params(&url).get("scope").is_some_and(|scope| scope == "1") {
  1045	                            reply["kind"] = json!(viewer.1); reply["scope"] = json!(viewer.2);
  1046	                        }
  1047	                        respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply)
  1048	                    },
  1049	                    Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) })),
  1050	                }
  1051	            },
  1052	            Err(refusal) => respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
  1053	        },
  1054	        (Method::Get, "/auth/config") => {
  1055	            let (auth, environment) = {
  1056	                let engine = producing_engine(shared);
  1057	                let environment = if shared.personas { "development" }
  1058	                    else if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging") { "staging" } else { "production" };
  1059	                (engine.schema().auth.clone(), environment)
  1060	            };
  1061	            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — an opt-in proof marker asserts the directly bound server, without granting authority.
  1062	            let mut config = json!({ "guests": auth.guests, "password": auth.password, "development": shared.personas, "devDoors": shared.dev_doors(), "environment": environment });
  1063	            if let Ok(id) = std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID") && !id.is_empty() {
  1064	                config["proof_id"] = json!(id);
  1065	            }
  1066	            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, config);
  1067	        }
  1068	        (Method::Get, "/") => {
  1069	            let seq = match shared.read_head() {
  1070	                Ok((_, seq)) => seq,
  1071	                Err(error) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) })),
  1072	            };
  1073	            let body = {
  1074	                let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
  1075	                let ops: Vec<Json> = bound.programs.iter().filter(|p| p.program.kind != snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job).map(|p| json!({ "name": p.program.name, "kind": p.program.kind.word(), "args": p.program.args })).collect();

   162	
   163	type Checked = (u64, Option<String>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>, Json);
   164	// @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one commit wakes every held poll; they share one answer per cursor.
   165	/// Wake answers by (cursor, published head, generation, store identity). Only
   166	/// a held poll woken by a publication asks here, and an answer is computed
   167	/// after reading that publication, so its snapshot is newer than the poll's
   168	/// entry and than anything its client knew when it asked. The answer names
   169	/// no viewer, so polls from the same cursor share one read: a commit costs one
   170	/// read per distinct cursor, not one per held poll. Entry and unheld polls
   171	/// read the store themselves (the published counter is a wake signal, never
   172	/// the source of truth). A shared answer is at most a second old and never
   173	/// below the cursor; failures are not kept.
   174	#[derive(Default)]
   175	pub(crate) struct Answers { entries: std::collections::VecDeque<((u64, u64, u64, Option<String>), Instant, Checked)> }
   176	const ANSWERS_KEPT: usize = 64;
   177	const ANSWER_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);
   178	
   179	fn answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   180	    let key = { let wake = lock(&shared.wake); (since, wake.seq, wake.generation, wake.store_id.clone()) };
   181	    let mut answers = lock(&shared.change_answers);
   182	    if let Some((_, _, hit)) = answers.entries.iter().find(|(k, at, hit)| *k == key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE && hit.0 >= since) {
   183	        return Ok(hit.clone());
   184	    }
   185	    let checked = read_answer(shared, since)?;
   186	    // Older publications are never asked for again.
   187	    answers.entries.retain(|(k, at, _)| k.1 >= key.1 && *k != key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE);
   188	    if answers.entries.len() >= ANSWERS_KEPT { answers.entries.pop_front(); }
   189	    answers.entries.push_back((key, Instant::now(), checked.clone()));
   190	    Ok(checked)
   191	}
   192	
   193	fn read_answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   194	    let _timing = super::latency::EngineSpan::new();
   195	    #[cfg(test)]
   196	    super::test_work(&shared.changes_refresh);
   197	    if let Ok((epoch, mut reader)) = shared.reader() {
   198	        // A pooled reader can predate an adoption that has committed but not
   199	        // yet replaced the pool. Only a success is kept; anything else is read
   200	        // again behind the engine, which waits for that adoption to finish.
   201	        if let Ok(checked) = check(&mut reader, since) {
   202	            lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   203	            return Ok(checked);
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   207	    match shared.open_reader(&engine) {
   208	        Ok(mut reader) => { drop(engine); check(&mut reader, since) }
   209	        // A memory store has no detached reader: read under the engine.
   210	        Err(_) => check(&mut engine, since),
   211	    }
   212	}
   213	
   214	/// The notification's head, identity and invalidations share one read snapshot.
   215	fn check(engine: &mut snapback4_core::engine::Engine, since: u64) -> Result<(u64, Option<String>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>, Json), snapback4_core::error::RuntimeError,
   216	> {
   217	    use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
   218	    let all_tables: Vec<_> = engine.schema().visible_tables().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect();
   219	    let schema = engine.schema().clone();
   220	    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
   221	    let head = tx.next_seq()? - 1;
   222	    let store_id = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
   223	    let changes = tx.commits_after(since, 500)?;
   224	    // Advancing to the actual head must not skip invalidation beyond the
   225	    // bounded commit page. Saturation conservatively invalidates every table.
   226	    let mut tables = if changes.last().is_some_and(|(seq, _)| *seq < head) { all_tables }
   227	        else { changes.into_iter().flat_map(|(_, tables)| tables).filter(|name| all_tables.contains(name)).collect() };
   228	    tables.sort();
   229	    tables.dedup();
   230	    let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(tx.conn(), &schema, since, head);
   231	    let changes = settlement_changes(tx.conn(), &schema, since, head);
   232	    tx.rollback()?;
   233	    Ok((head, store_id, tables, preserved, changes))
   234	}
   235

    18	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
    19	fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {

    21	    if !requested.exists() {
    22	        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    23	    }
    24	    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    25	    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    26	    Ok(dir)
    27	}
    28	
    29	/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
    30	/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
    31	pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    32	    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
    33	}
    34	fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    35	    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    36	    if database.exists() {
    37	        let store = owner
    38	            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
    39	            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    40	        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
    41	            return release
    42	                .parse()
    43	                .map(Some)
    44	                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
    45	        }
    46	    }
    47	    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    48	    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    49	    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
    50	        Ok(text) => text
    51	            .trim()
    52	            .parse()
    53	            .map(Some)
    54	            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
    55	        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
    56	        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    57	    }
    58	}
    59	pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    60	    let project = Project { root };
    61	    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
    62	        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    63	    }
    64	    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    65	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    66	        return Err(bound
    67	            .diagnostics
    68	            .iter()
    69	            .map(ToString::to_string)
    70	            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    71	            .join("\n"));
    72	    }
    73	    Ok(bound)
    74	}
    75	fn check_change(
    76	    dir: &Path,
    77	    previous: Option<u64>,
    78	    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    79	    options: &Options,
    80	    target_release: Option<u64>,
    81	) -> Result<(), String> {
    82	    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
    83	        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
    84	        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    85	        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
    86	        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
    87	        for line in lines {
    88	            eprintln!("{line}");
    89	        }
    90	    }
    91	    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
    92	        eprintln!(
    93	            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
    94	            dir.display()
    95	        );
    96	    }
    97	    Ok(())
    98	}
    99	
   100	fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
   101	    #[cfg(unix)]
   102	    {
   103	        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
   104	            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
   105	            &dir.join(".release-command"),
   210	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
   211	    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
   212	    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
   213	    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
   214	    match result {
   215	        Ok(()) => 0,
   216	        Err(error) => {
   217	            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
   218	            1
   219	        }
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	
   223	pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   224	    report(
   225	        "deploy",
   226	        (|| {
   227	            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
   228	            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
   229	            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
   230	            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
   231	            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
   232	            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
   233	            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
   234	            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
   235	            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
   236	            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
   237	                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
   238	            }
   239	            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   240	            let held = if checking {
   241	                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
   242	                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
   243	            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
   244	            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
   245	                current(&dir, owner)?
   246	            } else {
   247	                held["release"].as_u64()
   248	            };
   249	            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
   250	            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
   251	            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   252	            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
   253	            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
   254	            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   255	            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
   256	            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   257	            let source_hash = {
   258	                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
   259	                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
   260	                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   261	                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
   262	                for (name,text) in ordered {
   263	                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
   264	                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
   265	                }
   266	                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
   267	            };
   268	            for (name,text) in sources {
   269	                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
   270	                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
   271	                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   272	            }
   273	            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
   274	            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
   275	            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
   276	                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
   277	            } else { None };
   278	            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
   279	            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
   280	            if let Some(built) =
   281	                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
   282	            {
   283	                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
   284	                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   285	                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
   286	                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   287	            }
   288	            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   289	            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
   290	            if let Some(previous)=previous {
   291	                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
   292	                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   293	                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
   294	                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
   295	                    return Ok(());
   296	                }
   297	            }
   298	            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
   299	            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
   300	            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
   301	                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
   302	                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
   303	                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
   304	                return result;
   305	            }
   306	            let final_dir=loop {
   307	                n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
   308	                let path=releases.join(n.to_string());
   309	                match std::fs::create_dir(&path) {Ok(())=>break path,Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists=>continue,Err(e)=>return Err(e.to_string())}
   310	            };
   311	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
   312	            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   313	            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
   314	                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
   315	            }
   316	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
   317	            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
   318	                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
   319	            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   320	            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
   321	            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   322	            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
   323	                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
   324	                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   325	            let result=activate(
   326	                &dir,
   327	                _deployment.as_ref(),
   328	                n,
   329	                previous,
   330	                revision,
   331	                staged_bound,
   332	                false,
   333	                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
   334	                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
   335	                options,
   336	            );
   337	            match result {
   338	                Ok(()) => { if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");} }
   339	                Err(error) => {
   340	                    // Keep the source reserved even after a lost reply; committed history is authoritative.
   341	                    let matching_latest=latest(&dir).ok().filter(|id|crate::release_files::digest(&final_dir).ok()==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(id.to_string())).ok());
   342	                    let error=if error.contains("dormant names") && matching_latest.is_some() {
   343	                        format!("E_ADOPT: source matches latest; use `snapback4 rollback {} --to latest` to reactivate it; alternatively `snapback4 deploy {} --restore-dormant`. {error}",dir.display(),dir.display())
   344	                    } else {error};
   345	                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
   346	                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
   347	                        loop {
   348	                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
   349	                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
   350	                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
   351	                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
   352	                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
   353	                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
   354	                        }
   355	                        println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"code":"E_ADOPT","message":error,"dormant":inventory}));
   356	                    }
   357	                    return Err(error);
   358	                }
   359	            }
   360	            Ok(())
   361	        })(),
   362	    )
   363	}
   364	
   365	pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   366	    report(
   367	        "rollback",
   368	        (|| {
   369	            let dir = directory(options)?;
   370	            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
   371	            let _deployment = target(&dir)?;
   372	            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
   373	            let held = status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
   374	            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
   375	                current(&dir, owner)?
   376	            } else {
   377	                held["release"].as_u64()
   378	            }
   379	            .ok_or("no current release")?;
   380	            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);

    17	const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
    18	  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
    19	  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };

    21	async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
    22	  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
    23	  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
    24	  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
    25	  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
    26	  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
    27	  try {
    28	    const url = await ready(run, line => {
    29	      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
    30	      if (!match) return;
    31	      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
    32	      return match[2];
    33	    }, timeout);
    34	    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    35	    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    36	    let personas: string[] = [];
    37	    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
    38	      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
    39	      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
    40	      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
    41	      personas = status.body.personas;
    42	    }
    43	    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
    44	  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
    45	}
    46	export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
    47	  personas: string[];
    48	  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
    49	  now(): Promise<number>;
    50	}
    51	/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
    52	export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
    53	  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
    54	  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
    55	  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
    56	  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
    57	  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
    58	  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
    59	  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    60	    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    61	    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
    62	    return body.now;
    63	  } }));
    64	}
    65	export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
    66	  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;
    67	  seedAccounts(options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions>;
    68	  sessions?: StagingSessions;
    69	}
    70	export interface ServeOptions extends ProofOptions { seedUsers?: SeedAccountsOptions }
    71	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
    72	export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
    73	  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
    74	  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
    75	  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
    76	  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], opts);
    77	  const served: ServeHost = { ...host,
    78	    operatorToken: async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
    79	      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
    80	      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--release", directory, "--quiet"], opts);
    81	      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
    82	      const token = result.stdout.trim();
    83	      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof operator token malformed");
    84	      return token;
    85	    },
    86	    seedAccounts: accountOptions => seedAccounts(served, accountOptions),
    87	  };
    88	  try { if (opts.seedUsers) served.sessions = await served.seedAccounts(opts.seedUsers); return served; }
    89	  catch (error) { await served.stop(); throw error; }
    90	}
    91	async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {
    92	  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
    93	  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".");
    94	  const directory = resolve(cwd, dir);
    95	  // Vite is resolved from the app, never the proof package's development dependencies.
    96	  const vite = kind === "vite" ? createRequire(resolve(directory, "package.json")).resolve("vite") : "";
    97	  const run = logged(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-host.js", import.meta.url)), kind, directory, ports, vite], { ...options, env, detached: process.platform !== "win32", livenessPipe: true });
    98	  try {
    99	    const url = await ready(run, line => {
   100	      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_PROOF_READY (\{.*\})$/);
   101	      if (!match) return;
   102	      const data = JSON.parse(match[1]!);
   103	      if (data.pid !== run.child.pid || data.kind !== kind) throw Error("proof frontend PID mismatch");
   104	      return data.url as string;
   105	    }, timeout);
   106	    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
   107	    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
   108	    await response.body?.cancel();
   109	    return { url, pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
   110	  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
   111	}
   112	export function startStatic(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("static", dir, options); }
   113	export function startVite(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("vite", dir, options); }

   115	export interface CliResult { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: ProofHost["logs"] }
   116	/** Waits for close (stdio drained), never merges the two streams. Nonzero is returned for refusal assertions. */
   117	export function cli(args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
   118	  return command(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("../bin/snapback4.js", import.meta.url)), ...args], { ...options, secret: options.secret || args[0] === "token" });
   119	}
   120	/** A bounded directly spawned build/tool command, with the same file logs. */
   121	export async function command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
   122	  const run = logged(binary, args, { ...options, env: { ...process.env, ...options.env } });
   123	  try {
   124	    await bounded(run.closed, deadline(options.timeoutMs, 120_000), "proof cli close");
   125	    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
   126	    return { code: run.child.exitCode, signal: run.child.signalCode, stdout: run.stdout(), stderr: logText(run.logs.stderr), logs: run.logs };
   127	  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
   128	  finally { run.child.stdout?.destroy(); } // Secret capture must not pin the driver after a deadline.
   129	}
   130	async function successful(args: string[], options: ProofOptions) {
   131	  const result = await cli(args, options);
   132	  if (result.code !== 0) {
   133	    const stdout = result.stdout.trimEnd();
   134	    const tail = stdout.length > 4000 ? `…${stdout.slice(-4000)}` : stdout;
   135	    throw Error(`proof CLI failed (${result.code}): ${result.stderr}; stdout tail: ${tail || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stderr}`);
   136	  }
   137	  return result;
   138	}
   139	export function deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options: ProofOptions = {}) {
   140	  if (typeof schema !== "string" || !schema) throw Error(`proof deploy requires an explicit saved schema file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(schema) ?? String(schema)}: did npm run day2:proof pass versions/v1?`);
   141	  return successful(["deploy", dir, "--schema", schema, ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
   142	}
   143	export function rollback(dir: string, to: number | "latest", options: ProofOptions = {}) {
   144	  if (to !== "latest" && (!Number.isSafeInteger(to) || to < 1)) throw Error(`proof rollback requires an explicit immutable release target; got ${JSON.stringify(to) ?? String(to)}`);
   145	  return successful(["rollback", dir, "--to", String(to), ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
   146	}
   147	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — saved-source proof results expose authored cases separately from query sweeps.
   148	export interface TestSourceOptions extends ProofOptions { fixtureOnly?: boolean }
   149	export interface TestSourceResult extends CliResult { casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number }
   150	export function testSource(source: string, options: TestSourceOptions = {}): Promise<TestSourceResult> {
   151	  if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) throw Error(`proof testSource requires an explicit saved source file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(source) ?? String(source)}: pass versions/v1`);
   152	  const { fixtureOnly = false, ...proofOptions } = options;
   153	  return successful(["test", "--source", source, ...(proofOptions.args ?? [])], proofOptions).then(result => {
   154	    const summaries = [...result.stdout.matchAll(/^SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES (\{.*\})$/gm)];
   155	    if (summaries.length !== 1) throw Error(`proof testSource received no unique business-case summary; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
   156	    let cases: { run: number; skipped: number };
   157	    try { cases = JSON.parse(summaries[0]![1]!) as { run: number; skipped: number }; }
   158	    catch { throw Error(`proof testSource received a malformed business-case summary: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`); }
   159	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cases.run) || cases.run < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(cases.skipped) || cases.skipped < 0) {
   160	      throw Error(`proof testSource received invalid business-case counts: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
   161	    }
   162	    if (cases.run === 0 && !fixtureOnly) {
   163	      throw Error(`proof testSource ran no business cases (${cases.skipped} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl for this saved version, or pass { fixtureOnly: true } when fixture loading alone is the intended proof; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}`);
   164	    }
   165	    return { ...result, casesRun: cases.run, casesSkipped: cases.skipped };
   166	  });
   167	}

   171	}

   234	/** Returns the fresh nonempty text as a string, after it differs from previous. */
   235	export async function waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string | null, options: number | { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number } = 30_000): Promise<string> {
   236	  const timeoutMs = deadline(typeof options === "number" ? options : options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
   237	  let substring = substringLocators.get(locator);
   238	  if (typeof options !== "number" && options.within) {
   239	    locator = options.within.locator(locator);
   240	    if (substring) substring = options.within.locator(substring);
   241	  }
   242	  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
   243	  let count = 0, text: string | null = null, detail = "";
   244	  while (performance.now() < end) {
   245	    try {
   246	      count = await bounded(locator.count(), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator count");
   247	      text = count ? await bounded(locator.textContent({ timeout: Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, end - performance.now())) }), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator text") : null;
   248	      if (text && text !== previous) return text;
   249	    } catch (error) {
   250	      detail = error instanceof Error && (error.name === "TimeoutError" || /timed out after/.test(error.message))
   251	        ? "locator observation exceeded the remaining proof deadline" : String(error);
   252	      // A strictness/selector error needs its original repair, not a timeout.
   253	      if (error instanceof Error && error.name !== "TimeoutError" && !/timed out after/.test(error.message)) throw error;
   254	    }
   255	    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
   256	  }
   257	  const hint = count === 0 && substring && await bounded(substring.count(), 1000, "substring count").catch(() => 0)
   258	    ? "; a substring match exists; pass a RegExp or `exact: false`" : "";
   259	  throw Error(`no fresh signal after ${timeoutMs} ms: ${String(locator)}; last count=${count}, text=${JSON.stringify(text)}; ${count ? "present but unchanged" : "absent"}; previous=${JSON.stringify(previous)}${hint}${detail ? `; ${detail}` : ""}`);
   260	}
   261	/** Poll authoritative server truth after a receipt; a pending prediction is not a commit. */
   262	export async function waitForServer<T>(read: () => Promise<T>, accepts: (value: T) => boolean, options: { timeoutMs?: number; label?: string } = {}): Promise<T> {
   263	  const timeoutMs = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000), end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
   264	  let last: T | undefined;
   265	  while (performance.now() < end) {
   266	    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
   267	    try {
   268	      const value = await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(read), new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
   269	        timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`)), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()));
   270	      })]);
   271	      last = value;
   272	      if (accepts(value)) return value;
   273	    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
   274	    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
   275	  }
   276	  throw Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`);
   277	}
   278	export async function fillRN(locator: Locator, text: string): Promise<void> {
   279	  await locator.focus(); await locator.press("ControlOrMeta+A"); await locator.press("Backspace");
   280	  await locator.pressSequentially(text);
   281	}

   283	export async function selectByValue(locator: Locator, value: string): Promise<void> { await locator.selectOption({ value }); }

    29	    pub fn start(project: &Path, store: &str, address: std::net::SocketAddr, trace_empty: bool, detail: bool) -> Result<Self, String> {
    30	        Self::start_with_console(project, store, address, trace_empty, detail, Instant::now, std::io::stderr())
    31	    }
    32	    pub(super) fn start_with_console(
    33	        project: &Path, store: &str, address: std::net::SocketAddr, trace_empty: bool, detail: bool,
    34	        clock: impl Fn() -> Instant + Send + 'static, mut output: impl Write + Send + 'static,

    36	        if !component(store) { return Err("invalid store identity for dev evidence".into()); }

    50	        let writer_dropped = dropped.clone();
    51	        std::thread::Builder::new().name("dev-refusals".into()).spawn(move || {
    52	            crate::thread_priority::background();
    53	            // Holding this lock, not a PID or an old timestamp, proves liveness.
    54	            let _active = active;
    55	            let mut records = VecDeque::new();
    56	            let mut console = console::Console::with_detail(detail);
    57	            let mut shutdown = None;
    58	            let mut remaining = LIMIT;
    59	            loop {
    60	                let now = clock();
    61	                console.flush_due(now, &identity, &mut output);
    62	                // The marker follows already accepted records. Drain any
    63	                // racing handoffs too, bounded by the channel capacity; held
    64	                // requests need not release their sender clones.
    65	                let next = if shutdown.is_some() {
    66	                    if remaining == 0 { Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) }
    67	                    else { remaining -= 1; receiver.try_recv().map_err(|_| mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) }
    68	                } else { receiver.recv_timeout(console.wait(now)) };
    69	                let record = match next {
    70	                    Ok(Message::Record(record)) => record,
    71	                    Ok(Message::Shutdown(done)) => { shutdown = Some(done); continue; },
    72	                    Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => continue,
    73	                    Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => {
    74	                        console.flush_all(&identity, &mut output);
    75	                        break;
    76	                    }
    77	                };
    78	                let at = records.iter().position(|r: &Value| r["responseSequence"].as_u64() > record["responseSequence"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(records.len());
    79	                records.insert(at, record.clone());
    80	                if records.len() > LIMIT { records.pop_front(); }
    81	                if let Err(error) = save(&path, &identity, &records, writer_dropped.load(Ordering::Relaxed)) {
    82	                    console.push_at(json!({"operation":"local evidence writer", "code":"save failed",
    83	                        "clause":error.to_string()}), clock(), &identity, &mut output);
    84	                }
    85	                // EMPTY_READ is requested local evidence, never a refusal line.
    86	                if record["emptyRead"] != true {
    87	                    console.push_at(record, clock(), &identity, &mut output);
    88	                }
    89	            }
    90	            // Acknowledgement means records and console groups have been
    91	            // flushed and the live-instance lock released. No join or sender
    92	            // disconnection is needed, even with an outstanding held poll.
    93	            drop(_active);
    94	            if let Some(done) = shutdown { let _ = done.send(()); }
    95	        }).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    96	        Ok(Self { sender: Some(sender), closing: Default::default(), trace_empty, dropped, requests: Default::default(), watermark: Some(watermark) })
    97	    }
    98	    /// Only the server owner calls this after stopping admission. The single
    99	    /// deadline covers both a full queue and a blocked disk/stderr writer.
   100	    pub(super) fn shutdown(&self) -> bool {
   101	        let Some(sender) = &self.sender else { return true; };
   102	        self.closing.store(true, Ordering::Release);
   103	        let deadline = Instant::now() + SHUTDOWN_WAIT;
   104	        let (done, completed) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   105	        let mut message = Message::Shutdown(done);
   106	        loop {
   107	            match sender.try_send(message) {
   108	                Ok(()) => break,
   109	                Err(mpsc::TrySendError::Disconnected(_)) => return false,
   110	                Err(mpsc::TrySendError::Full(pending)) => message = pending,
   111	            }
   112	            if Instant::now() >= deadline { return false; }
   113	            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
   114	        }
   115	        completed.recv_timeout(deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now())).is_ok()
   116	    }
   117	    fn sequence(&self, mut record: Value) -> Value {
   118	        record["responseSequence"] = json!(self.watermark.as_ref().map_or(0, |w| w.next()));
   119	        record
   120	    }
   121	    /// A parked chunk is a terminal refusal without an HTTP refusal envelope.
   122	    /// Called only by dev maintenance after releasing the engine guard.
   123	    pub(super) fn job(&self, run: &snapback4_core::JobRun) {
   124	        if self.sender.is_none() { return; }
   125	        let code = run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").split(':').next().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN");
   126	        self.submit(self.sequence(json!({"operation":run.name, "jobId":run.id,
   127	            "code":code, "clause":"cause not instrumented", "at":snapback4_core::now_ms()})));
   128	    }
   129	    fn submit(&self, record: Value) {
   130	        if let Some(sender) = &self.sender {
   131	            // A stalled disk or stderr must never stall a request or grow memory.
   132	            if self.closing.load(Ordering::Acquire) || sender.try_send(Message::Record(record)).is_err() { self.dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); }
   133	        }
   134	    }
   135	}
   136	
   137	fn private_file(path: &Path) -> std::io::Result<File> {
   138	    let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new(); options.write(true).read(true).create_new(true);
   139	    #[cfg(unix)] { use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt; options.mode(0o600); }
   140	    options.open(path)
   141	}
   142	fn save(path: &Path, identity: &Value, records: &VecDeque<Value>, dropped: usize) -> std::io::Result<()> {
   143	    let temp = path.with_extension("tmp");
   144	    // This instance has one writer. A leftover replacement must not wedge it;
   145	    // unlinking also avoids following a stale symlink when opening the new file.
   146	    match fs::remove_file(&temp) { Ok(()) => {}, Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}, Err(e) => return Err(e) }
   147	    let mut file = private_file(&temp)?;
   148	    let mut envelope = identity.clone();
   149	    envelope["scope"] = json!(SCOPE);
   150	    envelope["records"] = json!(records);
   151	    envelope["dropped"] = json!(dropped);
   152	    let result = (|| { file.write_all(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&envelope)?.as_bytes())?; fs::rename(&temp, path) })();
   153	    if result.is_err() { let _ = fs::remove_file(temp); }
   154	    result
   155	}
   156	fn component(s: &str) -> bool { !s.is_empty() && s.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_') }
   157	
   158	/// Only locked, identity-matching streams belong to a currently running dev.
   159	/// Legacy project-global records and stopped processes are never selected.
   160	pub(crate) fn recent(data: &Path, store: Option<&str>, instance: Option<&str>) -> Result<Value, String> {
   161	    let root = data.join(DIRECTORY);
   162	    let mut candidates: Vec<(PathBuf, Value, File)> = Vec::new();
   163	    if root.is_dir() {
   164	        for directory in fs::read_dir(root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
   165	            let directory = directory.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   166	            let store_id = directory.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
   167	            if !component(&store_id) || store.is_some_and(|s| s != store_id) || !directory.path().is_dir() { continue; }
   168	            for entry in fs::read_dir(directory.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
   169	                let path = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.path();
   170	                if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) != Some("lock") { continue; }
   171	                let id = path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
   172	                if !component(id) || instance.is_some_and(|s| s != id) { continue; }
   173	                let active = File::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   174	                match active.try_lock_shared() {
   175	                    Ok(()) => continue,
   176	                    Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => {},
   177	                    Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
   178	                }
   179	                let identity: Value = serde_json::from_reader(&active).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   180	                if identity["storeId"] != store_id || identity["instance"] != id { return Err("mismatched dev instance identity".into()); }
   181	                candidates.push((path.with_extension("json"), identity, active));
   182	            }
   183	        }
   184	    }
   185	    if candidates.len() != 1 {
   186	        return Err(if candidates.is_empty() { "No live matching dev instance. Run snapback4 dev and reproduce the refusal; stale records are not selected.".into() }
   187	            else { format!("Multiple live dev instances; select `why recent --instance ID` (optionally --store ID): {}", candidates.iter().map(|(_,id,_)| id.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) });
   188	    }
   189	    let (path, identity, active) = candidates.pop().unwrap();
   190	    let envelope: Value = serde_json::from_reader(File::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   191	    let records = envelope["records"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dev refusal records")?;
   192	    if !identity.as_object().ok_or("invalid dev identity")?.iter().all(|(key, value)| envelope.get(key) == Some(value))
   193	        || records.len() > LIMIT || records.iter().any(|r| r["at"].as_i64().is_none_or(|t| t < identity["startedAt"].as_i64().unwrap_or(i64::MAX))) {
   194	        return Err("stale or mismatched dev refusal records".into());
   195	    }
   196	    let latest = watermark::Watermark::open(&path.with_extension("watermark")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.load();
   197	    let persisted = records.last().and_then(|r| r["responseSequence"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
   198	    if latest != persisted {
   199	        return Err(format!("Latest refusal evidence pending or dropped (response {latest}, persisted {persisted}); older records are not its cause. Retry after the dev writer recovers or reproduce the request."));
   200	    }
   201	    if !matches!(active.try_lock_shared(), Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock)) { return Err("dev instance stopped while reading records".into()); }
   202	    Ok(envelope)
   203	}

   212	            if full { for _ in 0..LIMIT { sender.try_send(Message::Record(Value::Null)).unwrap(); } }

   215	            assert!(!stream.shutdown());

   217	            assert!(began.elapsed() < SHUTDOWN_WAIT + Duration::from_secs(1));
   218	            // Receiver and sender remain alive throughout the bounded wait.
   219	            drop(receiver);
   220	        }
