     6	pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, operation: &str, body: &str) {
     7	    if body.len() > 64 * 1024 {
     8	        return respond_timed(
     9	            request,
    10	            shared,
    11	            400,
    12	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    13	        );
    14	    }
    15	    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    16	        Ok(p) => p,
    17	        Err(r) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)})),
    18	    };
    19	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — Following custody is outside the closed read allow-list.
    20	    // The outer guard refuses before body intake; retain the boundary here too.
    21	    if scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read {
    22	        let why = Refusal::new(
    23	            "E_READ_ONLY",
    24	            "auth",
    25	            "Following history requires a full session; ordinary inbox queries remain available",
    26	        )
    27	        .guide("read-only");
    28	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
    29	    }
    30	    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
    31	        Ok(v) => v,
    32	        Err(_) => {
    33	            return respond_timed(
    34	                request,
    35	                shared,
    36	                400,
    37	                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    38	            );
    39	        }
    40	    };
    41	    // Lock order is engine then controller. Authentication is checked again at
    42	    // the same engine incarnation that owns the source read transaction.
    43	    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    44	    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    45	    if let Err(r) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
    46	        drop(engine);
    47	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)}));
    48	    }
    49	    let Some(program) = engine.program(operation) else {
    50	        drop(engine);
    51	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"phase":"restart"}));
    52	    };
    53	    let Some(plan) = projection::plan(engine.schema(), program) else {
    54	        drop(engine);
    55	        return respond_timed(
    56	            request,
    57	            shared,
    58	            200,
    59	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_DECLARATION"}),
    60	        );
    61	    };
    62	    let binding = plan.binding;
    63	    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
    64	    let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(Json::as_object) else {
    65	        drop(engine);
    66	        return respond_timed(
    67	            request,
    68	            shared,
    69	            400,
    70	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    71	        );
    72	    };
    73	    if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,&Json::Object(fields.clone())) {
    74	        drop(engine);return respond_timed(request,shared,400,json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT","message":why.message,"denied":why}));
    75	    }
    76	    for (name, value) in fields {
    77	        let Some(value) = program
    78	            .args
    79	            .get(name)
    80	            .and_then(|kind| wire::from_json(kind, value).ok())
    81	        else {
    82	            drop(engine);
    83	            return respond_timed(
    84	                request,
    85	                shared,
    86	                400,
    87	                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    88	            );
    89	        };
    90	        args.insert(name.clone(), value);
    91	    }
    92	    let now = shared.now();
    93	    let mut reader = lock(&shared.following);
    94	    let action = payload
    95	        .get("action")
    96	        .and_then(Json::as_str)
    97	        .unwrap_or("unit");
    98	    let answer = if action == "open" {
    99	        reader
   100	            .opening_projected(&engine, operation, Context::new(&principal, args).at(now))
   101	            .map(|o| json!({"opening":o.request.opening}).to_string())
   102	    } else if let Some(opening) = payload.get("opening").and_then(Json::as_str) {
   103	        if action == "cancel" {
   104	            reader
   105	                .cancel_projected(
   106	                    &mut engine,
   107	                    opening,
   108	                    operation,
   109	                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   110	                )
   111	                .map(|_| json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
   112	        } else if action == "refresh" {
   113	            let mounted = serde_json::from_value::<
   114	                Vec<snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Mounted>,
   115	            >(payload.get("mounted").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])));
   116	            let seen = serde_json::from_value::<Vec<String>>(
   117	                payload.get("seen").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])),
   118	            );
   119	            match (mounted, seen) {
   120	                (Ok(mounted), Ok(seen)) => reader
   121	                    .refresh_slice_metered(
   122	                        &mut engine,
   123	                        opening,
   124	                        operation,
   125	                        Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   126	                        &mounted,
   127	                        &seen,
   128	                    )
   129	                    .map(|(r, _)| serde_json::to_string(&r).unwrap()),
   130	                _ => Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string()),
   131	            }
   132	        } else if action == "unit"
   133	            && payload
   134	                .get("token")
   135	                .is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null() && !v.is_string())
   136	        {
   137	            Ok(json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
   138	        } else if action == "unit" {
   139	            let q = UnitRequest {
   140	                declaration: binding.declaration,
   141	                page_size: binding.page_size,
   142	                opening: opening.into(),
   143	                token: payload
   144	                    .get("token")
   145	                    .and_then(Json::as_str)
   146	                    .map(str::to_owned),
   147	            };
   148	            reader
   149	                .request_projected(
   150	                    &mut engine,
   151	                    q,
   152	                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   153	                    operation,
   154	                )
   155	                .map(|out| out.answer.bytes().to_owned())
   156	        } else {
   157	            Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
   158	        }
   159	    } else {
   160	        Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
   161	    };
   162	    drop(reader);
   163	    drop(engine);
   164	    let bytes =
   165	        answer.unwrap_or_else(|e| json!({"phase":"refused","code":e.refusal().code}).to_string());
   166	    // Do not parse/re-encode a pinned response or attach a fresh store counter.
   167	    let mut response = Response::from_string(bytes);
   168	    if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
   169	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "application/json").unwrap());
   170	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "private, no-store").unwrap());
   171	    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()) {
   172	        response.add_header(header);
   173	    }
   174	    let _ = request.respond(response);
   175	}

     1	//! The online-only mutation route and a wake state independent of the writer.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use snapback4_core::engine::ephemeral::{Hub, Snapshot};

     5	pub(super) struct Wake {
     6	    pub seq: u64,
     7	    pub store_id: Option<String>,
     8	    pub generation: u64,
     9	    pub eph: Snapshot,
    10	}
    11	impl Wake {
    12	    pub fn new(engine: &Engine) -> Self {
    13	        Self {
    14	            seq: engine.seq().unwrap_or(0),
    15	            store_id: engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap_or(None),
    16	            generation: engine.generation(),
    17	            eph: engine.ephemeral_hub().snapshot().expect("new hub"),

    21	#[derive(Default)]
    22	pub(super) struct Rate {
    23	    buckets: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, (Instant, f64)>>,
    24	    #[cfg(test)]
    25	    fixed_now: Mutex<Option<Instant>>,
    26	}
    27	impl Rate {
    28	    fn now(&self) -> Instant {
    29	        #[cfg(test)]
    30	        if let Some(now) = *lock(&self.fixed_now) {
    31	            return now;
    32	        }
    33	        Instant::now()
    34	    }
    35	    #[cfg(test)]
    36	    pub(super) fn set_now(&self, now: Instant) {
    37	        *lock(&self.fixed_now) = Some(now);
    38	    }
    39	    pub fn admit(&self, key: String, now: Instant) -> bool {
    40	        let mut buckets = lock(&self.buckets);
    41	        buckets.retain(|_, (at, _)| now.saturating_duration_since(*at) < Duration::from_secs(60));
    42	        if !buckets.contains_key(&key) && buckets.len() >= 4096 {
    43	            return false;
    44	        }
    45	        let (at, tokens) = buckets.entry(key).or_insert((now, 40.0));
    46	        *tokens = (*tokens + now.saturating_duration_since(*at).as_secs_f64() * 20.0).min(40.0);
    47	        *at = now;
    48	        if *tokens < 1.0 {
    49	            false
    50	        } else {
    51	            *tokens -= 1.0;
    52	            true
    53	        }
    54	    }
    55	}
    56	impl Shared {
    57	    // @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — lock order engine → hub → wake.
    58	    pub(super) fn install_ephemeral_wake(self: &Arc<Self>) {
    59	        let owner = Arc::downgrade(self);
    60	        self.ephemeral.set_publisher(move |snapshot| {
    61	            if let Some(shared) = owner.upgrade() {
    62	                let mut wake = lock(&shared.wake);
    63	                wake.store_id = Some(snapshot.identity.clone());
    64	                wake.generation = snapshot.generation;
    65	                wake.eph = snapshot;
    66	                drop(wake);
    67	                shared.polls.notify_all();
    68	            }
    69	        }).expect("new hub publisher");
    70	    }
    71	    pub(super) fn durable_committed(&self, engine: &Engine) {
    72	        self.publish_durable(engine, true);
    73	    }
    74	    pub(super) fn publish_engine(&self, engine: &Engine) {
    75	        self.publish_durable(engine, false);
    76	    }
    77	    fn publish_durable(&self, engine: &Engine, committed: bool) {
    78	        if let Ok(seq) = engine.seq() {
    79	            let store_id = engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap_or(None);
    80	            let generation = engine.generation();
    81	            let mut current = lock(&self.seq);
    82	            let mut wake = lock(&self.wake);
    83	            let changed = wake.seq != seq || wake.store_id != store_id || wake.generation != generation;
    84	            *current = seq;
    85	            wake.seq = seq;
    86	            wake.store_id = store_id;
    87	            wake.generation = generation;
    88	            // The hub publisher alone owns wake.eph. Never wait for a running
    89	            // ephemeral program while holding the serving engine here.
    90	            if committed || changed {
    91	                self.polls.notify_all();
    92	                self.committed.notify_all();
    93	            }
    94	        }
    95	    }
    96	}
    97	
    98	pub(super) fn failed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, id: Option<&str>, why: Refusal) {
    99	    let wake = lock(&shared.wake);
   100	    let (store_id, head) = (wake.store_id.clone(), wake.seq);
   101	    drop(wake);
   102	    respond_timed(
   103	        request,
   104	        shared,
   105	        200,
   106	        super::mutation_reply(json!({"state":"failed","id":id,"why":refusal_json(&why),"store_id":store_id,"head":head})),
   107	    );
   108	}
   109	
   110	pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, op: &str, body: &str) {
   111	    let payload = serde_json::from_str::<Json>(if body.is_empty() { "{}" } else { body });
   112	    let id = payload
   113	        .as_ref()
   114	        .ok()
   115	        .and_then(|p| p.get("id"))
   116	        .and_then(Json::as_str)
   117	        .map(str::to_owned);
   118	    let failed = |request, why| failed(request, shared, id.as_deref(), why);
   119	    let payload = match payload {
   120	        Ok(p) => p,
   121	        Err(e) => {
   122	            return failed(
   123	                request,
   124	                Refusal::new(
   125	                    "E_INPUT",
   126	                    "input",
   127	                    format!("the request body is not JSON: {e}"),
   128	                ),
   129	            );
   130	        }
   131	    };
   132	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
   133	        Ok(p) => p.0,
   134	        Err(e) => return failed(request, e),
   135	    };
   136	    // A name may have been durable in a previous generation. Receipt custody
   137	    // precedes signature decoding, rate admission and ephemeral execution.
   138	    // @ref LLP 3000.022#6 — a stored success never executes a replacement body.
   139	    let fingerprint = match delivery_fingerprint(&payload) {
   140	        Ok(value) => value,
   141	        Err(why) => return failed(request, why),
   142	    };
   143	    {
   144	        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
   145	        let preflight = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal)
   146	            .and_then(|()| intent_store(&engine, &payload))
   147	            .and_then(|()| {
   148	                let args = payload.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or(json!({}));
   149	                id.as_deref().map(|id| engine.replay_mutation(&principal, id, op, &args))
   150	                    .transpose().map(Option::flatten).map_err(|e| e.refusal())
   151	            });
   152	        match preflight {
   153	            Ok(Some(out)) => {
   154	                let (store_id, head) = match response_head(&mut engine) {
   155	                    Ok(value) => value,
   156	                    Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":why})),
   157	                };
   158	                let mut reply = out.reply(id.as_deref());
   159	                reply["store_id"] = json!(store_id);
   160	                reply["head"] = json!(head);
   161	                return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply);
   162	            }
   163	            Err(why) => return failed(request, why),
   164	            Ok(None) => {
   165	                if let Err(error)=engine.require_non_media_ephemeral(&principal,id.as_deref()) { return failed(request,error.refusal()); }
   166	                // @ref LLP 3000#http-overload-diagnostics — an ID alone does not deduplicate ephemeral writes, including after schema adoption.
   167	                if payload.get("retry_write").and_then(Json::as_bool) == Some(true) {
   168	                    return failed(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "retry_write requires a durable mutation; refresh the operation signature"));
   169	                }
   170	                if let Err(error) = engine.schema().check_delivery(op, fingerprint.as_deref()) {
   171	                    return failed(request, error.refusal());
   172	                }
   173	            }
   174	        }
   175	    }
   176	    // Bearer digests identify sessions without retaining secrets. Dev personas
   177	    // have no session token and each principal is their development session.
   178	    let key = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request)
   179	        .map(|token| snapback4_core::value::digest(&token))
   180	        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("persona:{principal}"));
   181	    if !shared.ephemeral_rate.admit(key, shared.ephemeral_rate.now()) {
   182	        let mut why = Refusal::new(
   183	            "E_RATE_LIMIT",
   184	            "auth",
   185	            "ephemeral writes allow 20 requests per second, burst 40; retry later",
   186	        )
   187	        .guide("ephemeral");
   188	        why.retryable = true;
   189	        return failed(request, why);
   190	    }

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! Assets on the wire: `POST /assets` takes the bytes and answers with the
     2	//! asset's facts; `GET /assets/<id>` serves them to a viewer the home row's
     3	//! read rule admits (LLP 1006 carried into Snapback 4). A one-view image is
     4	//! served once per principal and never cached anywhere; a deleted, cleared
     5	//! or expired home stops the bytes at the server. A `file` (LLP 3000.038)
     6	//! is uploaded with `?as=file&name=` and its declared `Content-Type`, which
     7	//! the server verifies against the bytes; it is only ever served as an
     8	//! attachment under `nosniff` and a sandboxing CSP.
     9	
    10	use crate::serve::{Shared, cors, producing_engine, principal_of, refusal_json, respond};
    11	use serde_json::json;
    12	use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
    13	use std::io::{self, Read as _, Seek, SeekFrom};
    14	use tiny_http::{Header, Request, Response};
    15	
    16	/// The most an upload may be when no column bounds it yet: the request is
    17	/// refused before the schema is consulted.
    18	pub const UPLOAD_CEILING: usize = 64 << 20;
    19	
    20	pub struct UploadPermit<'a>(&'a std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize);
    21	pub fn admit(shared: &Shared) -> Option<UploadPermit<'_>> {
    22	    use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
    23	    shared.uploads.fetch_update(Ordering::AcqRel, Ordering::Acquire, |n| (n < 2).then_some(n + 1)).ok()?;
    24	    Some(UploadPermit(&shared.uploads))
    25	}
    26	impl Drop for UploadPermit<'_> {
    27	    fn drop(&mut self) { self.0.fetch_sub(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release); }
    28	}

    30	/// Whether `POST /assets` carries `as=file`.
    31	pub fn is_file_upload(url: &str) -> bool {
    32	    crate::serve::query_params(url).get("as").is_some_and(|kind| kind == "file")
    33	}

    35	/// The most bytes a file upload may stream: the schema's largest file
    36	/// bound, under the general upload ceiling. A schema with no file column
    37	/// refuses before any body is read.
    38	pub fn file_ceiling(shared: &Shared) -> Result<usize, Refusal> {
    39	    match producing_engine(shared).file_upload_ceiling() {
    40	        Some(max) => Ok((max as usize).min(UPLOAD_CEILING)),
    41	        None => Err(Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "no column of this backend takes a file; declare one (`doc: file <=10000000`)").guide("assets")),
    42	    }
    43	}

    45	pub fn upload(request: Request, body: Vec<u8>, shared: &Shared) {
    46	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    47	        Ok(principal) => principal.0,
    48	        Err(refusal) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
    49	    };
    50	    if body.is_empty() {
    51	        return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "the upload is empty: POST the file's bytes as the body").guide("assets")) }));
    52	    }
    53	    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    54	    let file = match params.get("as").map(String::as_str) {
    55	        None | Some("image" | "video") => None,
    56	        Some("file") => Some(params.get("name").cloned().unwrap_or_default()),
    57	        Some(other) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("as={other} is not an upload kind; use as=file for a document, or omit it for an image or video")).guide("assets")) })),
    58	    };
    59	    let declared = match crate::serve::single_header(&request, "content-type") {
    60	        Ok(value) => snapback4_core::assets::declared_type(value),
    61	        Err(()) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "send one Content-Type header").guide("assets")) })),
    62	    };
    63	    let intent = params.get("intent").map(String::as_str);
    64	    if intent.is_some_and(|id| id.is_empty() || id.len() > 128 || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')) {
    65	        return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "intent is 1–128 ASCII letters, digits, hyphens or underscores")}));
    66	    }
    67	    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    68	    let fingerprint = snapback4_core::value::digest(&(format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&body)), &file.as_ref().map(|name| snapback4_core::assets::sanitize_name(name)), &declared));
    69	    let expected_store = params.get("store_id");
    70	    if intent.is_some() && expected_store.is_none() { return respond(request,400,json!({"denied":Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","intent uploads require store_id from the identified backend")})); }
    71	    let stored = (|| {
    72	        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
    73	            if producing_engine(shared).store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) {
    74	                return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_STORE_REPLACED", message: "upload intent belongs to another backend store".into() });
    75	            }
    76	        }
    77	        if let Some(id) = intent {
    78	            if let Some(asset) = producing_engine(shared).asset_intent(&principal, id, &fingerprint)? { return Ok(asset); }
    79	        }
    80	        let placement=if let Some(id)=intent {Some(producing_engine(shared).asset_declaration(&principal,id)?.ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_INTENT",message:"declare this intent's immutable placement before sending its bytes".into()} )?)}else{None};
    81	        let upload = match &file {
    82	            // @ref LLP 3000.038#2 — the declared type is verified against the bytes before admission.
    83	            Some(name) => {
    84	                let content_type = snapback4_core::assets::verify_file(&body, declared.as_deref())?;
    85	                producing_engine(shared).prepare_file(&principal, content_type, name, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
    86	            }
    87	            None => {
    88	                let sniffed = snapback4_core::assets::sniff(&body)?;
    89	                producing_engine(shared).prepare_asset(&principal, sniffed, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
    90	            }
    91	        };
    92	        #[cfg(test)] crate::serve::test_work(&shared.upload_work);
    93	        let upload = if let Some(id) = intent { upload.with_intent(id, &fingerprint).with_placement(placement.unwrap()) } else { upload };
    94	        let upload = upload.write(&body)?;
    95	        // Logout while disk I/O was in flight cannot publish a new asset.
    96	        principal_of(&request, shared).map_err(|_| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth("sign in again".into()))?;
    97	        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
    98	        crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    99	        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
   100	            if engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"upload store changed during disk write".into()}); }
   101	        }
   102	        engine.finish_asset(upload)
   103	    })();
   104	    match stored {
   105	        Ok(asset) => {
   106	            let mut facts = json!({ "id": asset.id, "type": asset.content_type, "bytes": asset.bytes, "width": asset.width, "height": asset.height, "duration": asset.duration });
   107	            if let Some(name) = asset.name { facts["name"] = json!(name); }
   108	            respond(request, 200, json!({ "asset": facts }))
   109	        }
   110	        Err(error) => {
   111	            let refusal = error.refusal();
   112	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{} at POST /assets: {}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
   113	            respond(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }))
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	}
   117	
   118	pub fn serve(request: Request, id: &str, shared: &Shared) {
   119	    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
   120	        Ok(capability) => capability,
   121	        Err(refusal) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
   122	    };
   123	    if id.is_empty() || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
   124	        return respond(request, 404, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_UNKNOWN", "asset", "no asset is served under that id to this viewer").guide("assets")) }));
   125	    }
   126	    let _transfer = shared.asset_transfers.enter(id, &principal);
   127	    let range_requested = request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("range"));
   128	    let granted = {
   129	        let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
   130	        let _persona_authority = match crate::serve::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
   131	            Ok(authority) => authority,
   132	            Err(why) => {
   133	            drop(engine); return respond(request, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
   134	            }
   135	        };
   136	        crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
   137	        engine.asset_access_request(&principal, id, shared.now(), range_requested)
   138	    };
   139	    let grant = match granted {
   140	        Ok(grant) => grant,
   141	        Err(error) => {
   142	            let refusal = error.refusal();
   143	            let status = match refusal.code.as_str() {
   144	                "E_ASSET_VIEWED" => 410,
   145	                "E_ASSET_RANGE" => 416,
   146	                "E_ASSET_UNKNOWN" => 404,
   147	                _ if refusal.retryable => 503,
   148	                _ => 500,
   149	            };
   150	            return respond(request, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
   151	        }
   152	    };
   153	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.5 — refuse only an otherwise successful once grant,
   154	    // after authorization/range/viewed checks and before opening any bytes.
   155	    if grant.once && scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read {
   156	        return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_ASSET_READ_ONLY", "asset", "this read session cannot consume a view-once asset").guide("read-only")}));
   157	    }
   158	    let Ok(mut file) = std::fs::File::open(&grant.path) else {
   159	        return respond(request, 404, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_UNKNOWN", "asset", "the bytes are gone").guide("assets")) }));
   160	    };
   161	    let total = grant.bytes;
   162	    let range = request.headers().iter().find(|h| h.field.as_str().as_str().eq_ignore_ascii_case("range"))
   163	        .map(|h| byte_range(h.value.as_str(), total)).unwrap_or(ByteRange::Ignore);
   164	    if range == ByteRange::Unsatisfiable {
   165	        let mut response = Response::empty(416);
   166	        if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
   167	        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Range", format!("bytes */{total}")).unwrap());
   168	        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "private, no-store").unwrap());
   169	        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff").unwrap());
   170	        for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(crate::serve::latency::header()) { response.add_header(header); }
   171	        let _ = request.respond_checked_with_completion(response, |_| crate::serve::latency::finished(true));
   172	        return;
   173	    }
   174	    let attachment = grant.kind == snapback4_core::assets::AssetKind::File;
   175	    let content_type = if attachment && grant.content_type.starts_with("text/") { format!("{}; charset=utf-8", grant.content_type) } else { grant.content_type.clone() };
   176	    let mut headers = vec![
   177	        Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", content_type.as_str()).unwrap(),
   178	        Header::from_bytes("Accept-Ranges", if grant.once { "none" } else { "bytes" }).unwrap(),
   179	        Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "private, no-store").unwrap(),
   180	        Header::from_bytes("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff").unwrap(),
   181	        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "content-length, content-range, accept-ranges, content-disposition, x-snapback-asset-home, x-snapback-generation").unwrap(),
   182	    ];
   183	    // @ref LLP 3000.038#5 — a file is always a download: never rendered inline, never sniffed, nothing active even if navigated to.
   184	    if attachment {
   185	        let name = grant.name.as_deref().unwrap_or("file");
   186	        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Content-Disposition", snapback4_core::assets::attachment_disposition(name)).unwrap());
   187	        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'none'; sandbox").unwrap());
   188	    }
   189	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.5 — only the download's ledger home authorizes retention.
   190	    if let Some((table, row, column)) = &grant.home {
   191	        let receipt = json!({"store_id":grant.store_id,"table":table,"row":row,"column":column}).to_string();
   192	        // Percent-encoded UTF-8 keeps arbitrary row identities safe in an ASCII header.
   193	        let encoded: String = receipt.bytes().map(|byte| format!("%{byte:02X}")).collect();
   194	        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Asset-Home", encoded).unwrap());
   195	    }
   196	    headers.extend(cors(&request));
   197	    headers.extend(crate::serve::latency::header());
   198	    let (status, start, length) = match range {
   199	        ByteRange::Satisfiable(start, end) => {
   200	            headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Content-Range", format!("bytes {start}-{end}/{total}")).unwrap());
   201	            (206, start, end - start + 1)
   202	        }
   203	        ByteRange::Ignore => (200, 0, total),
   204	        ByteRange::Unsatisfiable => unreachable!("handled above"),
   205	    };
   206	    if start > 0 && file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(start)).is_err() {
   207	        return respond(request, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_STORE", "store", "cannot read the bytes")) }));
   208	    }
   209	    let reader = ExactReader { inner: file.take(length), remaining: length };
   210	    let mut response = Response::new(tiny_http::StatusCode(status), headers, reader, Some(length as usize), None);
   211	    if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
   212	    if status == 206 {
   213	        response = response.with_status_code(206);
   214	    }
   215	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.7 — finish before reader disposal and one-view bookkeeping.
   216	    let delivered = request.respond_checked_with_completion(response, |result| crate::serve::latency::finished(result.is_err()));
   217	    if grant.once && delivered.is_ok() {
   218	        if let Err(error) = producing_engine(shared).record_asset_view(&principal, id, shared.now()) {
   219	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: one-view asset {id} was delivered to {principal}, but its view could not be recorded: {error}");
   220	        }
   221	    }
   222	}
   223	
   224	/// `io::copy` considers an early EOF successful. Convert it to an error so a
   225	/// truncated asset cannot consume a one-view grant after a short response.
   226	struct ExactReader<R> {
   227	    inner: R,
   228	    remaining: u64,
   229	}
   230	
   231	impl<R: io::Read> io::Read for ExactReader<R> {
   232	    fn read(&mut self, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
   233	        if self.remaining == 0 || buffer.is_empty() {
   234	            return Ok(0);
   235	        }
   236	        let wanted = buffer.len().min(self.remaining as usize);
   237	        let read = self.inner.read(&mut buffer[..wanted])?;
   238	        if read == 0 {
   239	            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof, "the asset ended before its recorded byte count"));
   240	        }
   241	        self.remaining -= read as u64;
   242	        Ok(read)
   243	    }
   244	}
   245	
   246	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   247	enum ByteRange {
   248	    Ignore,
   249	    Unsatisfiable,
   250	    Satisfiable(u64, u64),
   251	}
   252	
   253	/// One `bytes=a-b` range, clamped. A valid interval outside the file is
   254	/// distinguished from malformed syntax so the former receives HTTP 416.
   255	fn byte_range(header: &str, total: u64) -> ByteRange {
   256	    let Some((unit, spec)) = header.trim().split_once('=') else { return ByteRange::Ignore; };
   257	    if !unit.eq_ignore_ascii_case("bytes") || spec.contains(',') { return ByteRange::Ignore; }
   258	    let Some((start, end)) = spec.split_once('-') else { return ByteRange::Ignore; };
   259	    if end.contains('-') { return ByteRange::Ignore; }
   260	    let parse = |part: &str| -> Result<Option<u64>, ()> {

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! A request scope plus a response-layer backstop, never a caller channel.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — empty viewer-roster advice is private dev evidence only.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.015#2.1 — missing instrumentation must supersede older evidence.
     4	use super::{Recent, Sites};
     5	use serde_json::{Value, json};
     6	use snapback4_core::{Context, engine::refusal_trace::RefusalTrace};
     7	use std::{cell::RefCell, sync::{Arc, atomic::Ordering}};
     8	
     9	thread_local! { static CURRENT: RefCell<Option<Evidence>> = const { RefCell::new(None) }; }
    10	struct Evidence {
    11	    recent: Recent,
    12	    operation: String,
    13	    request: u64,
    14	    query: bool,
    15	    trace: RefusalTrace,
    16	    sites: Arc<Sites>,
    17	    generation: Option<u64>,
    18	    empty_rosters: std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, String), Vec<String>>,
    19	    program_rosters: std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, String), Vec<String>>,
    20	}
    21	pub(in crate::serve) struct RequestScope;
    22	impl Drop for RequestScope { fn drop(&mut self) { CURRENT.with(|c| c.borrow_mut().take()); } }
    23	pub(in crate::serve) fn begin(shared: &crate::serve::Shared, path: &str) -> Option<RequestScope> {
    24	    if !shared.personas { return None; }
    25	    let recent = shared.refusals.clone();
    26	    let request = recent.requests.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed) + 1;
    27	    CURRENT.with(|c| *c.borrow_mut() = Some(Evidence { recent,
    28	        operation: path.strip_prefix("/q/").or_else(|| path.strip_prefix("/m/")).unwrap_or(path).into(),
    29	        request, query: path.starts_with("/q/"), trace: Default::default(), sites: Default::default(), generation: None, empty_rosters: Default::default(), program_rosters: Default::default() }));
    30	    Some(RequestScope)
    31	}
    32	pub(in crate::serve) fn context(context: Context, sites: Arc<Sites>, engine: &snapback4_core::Engine) -> Context {
    33	    CURRENT.with(|c| {
    34	        let mut c = c.borrow_mut();
    35	        let Some(evidence) = c.as_mut() else { return context };
    36	        evidence.sites = sites;
    37	        evidence.generation = Some(engine.generation());
    38	        // One row at most, only on the private dev evidence path; never expose
    39	        // physical roster emptiness to a caller or charge its query meter.
    40	        let mut empty = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    41	        for (table, definition) in engine.schema().visible_tables() {
    42	            for (phase, rule) in [("read rule", &definition.rules.read), ("insert rule", &definition.rules.insert), ("update rule", &definition.rules.update), ("delete rule", &definition.rules.delete)] {
    43	                let mut rosters = Vec::new();
    44	                viewer_rosters(rule, &mut rosters);
    45	                rosters.retain(|roster| *empty.entry(roster.clone()).or_insert_with(|| engine.store().rows_limited(roster, 0).is_ok_and(|(_, more)| !more)));
    46	                evidence.empty_rosters.insert((table.clone(), phase.into()), rosters);
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        if let Some(program) = engine.program(&evidence.operation) {
    50	            program_rosters(&program.body, &mut Default::default(), &mut evidence.program_rosters);
    51	            for rosters in evidence.program_rosters.values_mut() {
    52	                rosters.retain(|roster| *empty.entry(roster.clone()).or_insert_with(|| engine.store().rows_limited(roster, 0).is_ok_and(|(_, more)| !more)));
    53	            }
    54	        }
    55	        context.with_refusal_trace(evidence.trace.clone())
    56	    })
    57	}
    58	pub(in crate::serve) struct Pending { recent: Recent, record: Value }
    59	impl Drop for Pending {
    60	    fn drop(&mut self) { self.recent.submit(std::mem::take(&mut self.record)); }
    61	}
    62	// Inspect the actual encoded envelope, after overflow substitution. This covers
    63	// every responder using the shared JSON response layer, including receipt replay,
    64	// static admission and future paths with no attached collector. No user data is
    65	// copied into the record. Unknown empty answers enter only the opt-in local
    66	// trace; successful visibility filtering is never a refusal.
    67	pub(in crate::serve) fn response(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<Pending> {
    68	    CURRENT.with(|c| {
    69	        let mut c = c.borrow_mut();
    70	        let evidence = c.as_mut()?;
    71	        let body: Value = serde_json::from_slice(bytes).ok()?;
    72	        if evidence.query && evidence.trace.hidden() > 0 {
    73	            eprintln!("dev query: {} hidden: {} (encountered row images filtered by read rules)", evidence.operation, evidence.trace.hidden());
    74	        }
    75	        let code = body.get("denied").or_else(|| body.get("why")).and_then(|v| v.get("code")).and_then(Value::as_str);
    76	        let traced = evidence.trace.take();
    77	        let why = body.get("denied").or_else(|| body.get("why"));
    78	        // The one filtered read that is evidence about a program refusal: the
    79	        // refusing `t[x] ! else CODE` lookup itself, whose read rule hid the row.
    80	        let hidden = traced.as_ref().filter(|cause| !cause.terminal && cause.phase == "read rule").and_then(|cause| {
    81	            let site = why?.get("site")?.as_str()?;
    82	            evidence.sites.contains_key(&(cause.table.clone(), format!("lookup {site}"))).then(|| (cause.table.clone(), evidence.sites.get(&(cause.table.clone(), "read rule".into())).cloned()))
    83	        });
    84	        let program_hint = why.and_then(|why| Some((why.get("code")?.as_str()?.to_owned(), why.get("site")?.as_str()?.to_owned())))
    85	            .and_then(|key| evidence.program_rosters.get(&key)).filter(|tables| !tables.is_empty());
    86	        let roster_hint = program_hint.map(|tables| tables.iter().map(|table| format!("no rows in {table}: seed it with /dev/reset")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")).or_else(|| traced.as_ref().filter(|cause| cause.terminal).and_then(|cause| {
    87	            // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#11 — image labels retain the original rule's seed hint.
    88	            evidence.empty_rosters.get(&(cause.table.clone(), cause.phase.clone()))
    89	                .or_else(|| evidence.empty_rosters.get(&(cause.table.clone(), cause.clause.clone())))
    90	                .filter(|tables| !tables.is_empty())
    91	                .map(|tables| tables.iter().map(|table| format!("no rows in {table}: seed it with /dev/reset")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; "))
    92	        }));
    93	        let cause = traced.filter(|cause| {
    94	            // A filtered read earlier in a program is not evidence about a
    95	            // later uninstrumented terminal refusal in that same request.
    96	            code.is_none() || cause.terminal
    97	        });
    98	        let empty_answer = code.is_none();
    99	        let code = match code {
   100	            Some(code) => code,
   101	            None if evidence.query && body.get("data").is_some() && body.get("loading") != Some(&Value::Bool(true)) => {
   102	                // Visibility filtering is a successful read, not a refusal.
   103	                if cause.as_ref().is_some_and(|cause| !cause.terminal && !cause.no_candidate) { return None; }
   104	                if evidence.recent.trace_empty && empty(&body["data"]) { "EMPTY_READ" }
   105	                else { return None; }
   106	            }
   107	            None => return None,
   108	        };
   109	        let preflight = code == "E_AUTH" && evidence.generation.is_none() && why.and_then(|v| v.get("site")).is_none();
   110	        let mut record = json!({"preflight":preflight,"operation":evidence.operation, "code":code, "requestId":evidence.request,
   111	            "generation":evidence.generation, "clause":"cause not instrumented", "at":snapback4_core::now_ms()});
   112	        // A program's own `require … else CODE` (or `! else CODE`) carries
   113	        // its source site in the refusal itself: the response's own
   114	        // evidence, not an older trace, so the log can name the line.
   115	        if cause.is_none() && let Some(why) = why
   116	            && why.get("rule").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(code) && let Some(site) = why.get("site").and_then(Value::as_str) {
   117	            let kind = evidence.sites.get(&("$program".into(), site.into())).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("program refusal");
   118	            let kind = if kind == "program require" { if evidence.query { "query require" } else { "mutation require" } } else { kind };
   119	            record["clause"] = json!(kind);
   120	            record["ruleText"] = json!(evidence.sites.get(&("$clause".into(), site.into())));
   121	            record["site"] = json!(site);
   122	            record["siteKind"] = json!("program");
   123	            if let Some((table, rule)) = &hidden {
   124	                record["hiddenBy"] = json!({"table": table, "readRuleSite": rule});
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	        if empty_answer {
   128	            record["emptyRead"] = json!(true);
   129	            record["cause"] = json!(if cause.as_ref().is_some_and(|cause| cause.no_candidate) { "no-candidate" } else { "unrecorded" });
   130	            record["clause"] = json!("no probe miss and no rule cause were recorded");
   131	        }
   132	        if let Some(cause) = cause {
   133	            let clause = evidence.sites.get(&(cause.table.clone(), cause.phase.clone()))
   134	                .or_else(|| evidence.sites.get(&(cause.table.clone(), cause.clause.clone())));
   135	            let site = clause.or_else(|| evidence.sites.get(&(cause.table.clone(), "table".into())));
   136	            // Whether the site is the clause's own line or the table's (an omitted rule).
   137	            record["siteKind"] = json!(if clause.is_some() { "clause" } else if site.is_some() { "table" } else { "" });
   138	            record["table"] = json!(cause.table);
   139	            record["clause"] = json!(cause.clause);
   140	            record["phase"] = json!(cause.phase);
   141	            record["ruleText"] = json!(site.and_then(|site| evidence.sites.get(&("$clause".into(), site.clone()))));
   142	            record["site"] = json!(site);
   143	            record["executionSite"] = json!(cause.site);
   144	            record["generation"] = json!(cause.generation);
   145	            record["readRuleSite"] = json!(evidence.sites.get(&(cause.table, "read rule".into())));
   146	        }
   147	        if !empty_answer && let Some(hint) = roster_hint { record["seedHint"] = json!(hint); }
   148	        // Published before sending: why refuses an old disk snapshot even when
   149	        // the writer is blocked, this handoff drops, or responses finish out of order.
   150	        Some(Pending { recent: evidence.recent.clone(), record: evidence.recent.sequence(record) })
   151	    })
   152	}
   153	fn empty(value: &Value) -> bool {
   154	    match value {
   155	        Value::Null => true,
   156	        Value::Array(values) => values.is_empty(),
   157	        _ => false,
   158	    }
   159	}
   160	
   161	fn viewer_rosters(rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
   162	    use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
   163	    match rule {
   164	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, key, equalities, .. } if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).any(|term| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Viewer)) => { if !out.contains(table) { out.push(table.clone()); } }
   165	        RuleExpr::And(parts) | RuleExpr::Or(parts) => { for part in parts { viewer_rosters(part, out); } }
   166	        // A negated roster is not an admission gate.
   167	        _ => {},
   168	    }
   169	}
   170	
   171	fn expression_rosters(expression: &snapback4_core::ir::Expr, names: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>) -> Vec<String> {
   172	    use snapback4_core::ir::{Expr, Input, BinaryOp};
   173	    match expression {
   174	        Expr::Exists(read) | Expr::Get(read) if read.key.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer))) => vec![read.table.clone()],
   175	        Expr::Variable(name) => names.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_default(),
   176	        // Extra named equalities lower exists to a row temporary, a non-null
   177	        // guard, then the residual comparisons. Track that positive guard.
   178	        Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left, right } if matches!(right.as_ref(), Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null)) => expression_rosters(left,names),
   179	        Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left, right } if matches!(left.as_ref(), Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null)) => expression_rosters(right,names),
   180	        Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And | BinaryOp::Or, left, right } => {

   154	impl Shared {
   155	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — current adopted auth policy closes test authority, independently of dev mode.
   156	    pub(crate) fn dev_doors(&self) -> bool {
   157	        if !self.personas { return false; }
   158	        let bound = lock(&self.bound);
   159	        self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
   160	    }
   161	    // Revalidate under the writer: neither a slow body nor an adoption queue
   162	    // may carry fixture authority into a newly password-only schema.
   163	    pub(crate) fn dev_authority(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
   164	        if self.personas && (self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(engine.schema().auth.password && !engine.schema().auth.guests)) { Ok(()) }
   165	        else { Err(dev_doors::closed()) }
   166	    }
   167	    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 { lock(&self.clock).now().max(self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire)) }
   168	    pub(crate) fn refresh_time_audience_clock(&self, engine: &Engine, now: i64) {
   169	        if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
   170	            let durable = engine.store().meta("time_audience_clock").ok().flatten()
   171	                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN);
   172	            self.time_audience_clock.fetch_max(now.max(durable), Ordering::AcqRel);
   173	        } else {
   174	            self.time_audience_clock.store(i64::MIN, Ordering::Release);
   175	        }
   176	    }
   177	    fn advance_time_audiences(&self) -> Result<i64, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   178	        let requested = lock(&self.clock).now();
   179	        // Non-timed reader traffic must retain its independent reader lane,
   180	        // including while adoption or a mutation deliberately holds the writer.
   181	        if self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire) == i64::MIN { return Ok(requested); }
   182	        let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
   183	        let before = engine.seq()?;
   184	        let now = engine.advance_time_audiences_at(requested)?;
   185	        self.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, now);
   186	        if engine.seq()? != before { self.durable_committed(&engine); }
   187	        Ok(now)
   188	    }
   189	    fn next_time_audience(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   190	        if self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire) == i64::MIN { return Ok(None); }
   191	        lock(&self.engine).next_time_audience_at()
   192	    }
   193	    fn time_audience_delay(&self, at: i64) -> Duration {
   194	        let raw = lock(&self.clock).now();
   195	        let floor = self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire);
   196	        Duration::from_millis(if at <= floor { 0 } else { at.saturating_sub(raw).max(0) }.try_into().unwrap_or(u64::MAX))
   197	    }
   198	    pub(crate) fn worker_owner(&self) -> Option<Arc<std::fs::File>> { self.owner.as_ref().map(|owner| owner.worker_lock()) }
   199	}

   208	/// A panicking request must not turn one poisoned process mutex into a
   209	/// permanent server-wide panic loop. The guarded value is retained, the
   210	/// poison marker is cleared, and the next request can make progress.
   211	pub(crate) fn lock<T: Lockable>(mutex: &T) -> T::Guard<'_> {
   212	    mutex.recover_lock()
   213	}

   215	#[derive(Debug, Default)]
   216	pub struct AssetTransfers {
   217	    active: Mutex<BTreeSet<(String, String)>>,
   218	    available: Condvar,
   219	}
   220	
   221	impl AssetTransfers {
   222	    pub(crate) fn enter<'a>(&'a self, asset: &str, principal: &str) -> AssetTransfer<'a> {
   223	        let key = (asset.to_owned(), principal.to_owned());
   224	        let mut active = lock(&self.active);
   225	        while active.contains(&key) {
   226	            active = self.available.wait(active).unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner());
   227	        }
   228	        active.insert(key.clone());
   229	        AssetTransfer { transfers: self, key }
   230	    }
   231	}
   232	
   233	pub(crate) struct AssetTransfer<'a> {
   234	    transfers: &'a AssetTransfers,
   235	    key: (String, String),
   236	}
   237	
   238	impl Drop for AssetTransfer<'_> {
   239	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   240	        lock(&self.transfers.active).remove(&self.key);
   241	        self.transfers.available.notify_all();
   242	    }
   243	}

   245	/// A connection lasts as long as its poll and a grace after it: a client
   246	/// re-polls within milliseconds of an answer, a dropped one never does.
   247	use snapback4_core::engine::{PRESENCE_GRACE, PRESENCE_CONFIRMATION, PRESENCE_REFRESH};
   248	
   249	/// The registry of open long polls, per principal: how many are open now,
   250	/// and when the last one ended. A device that holds `/changes?wait` open
   251	/// (every tier does) is connected; nothing else is.
   252	#[derive(Debug)]
   253	pub struct Live {
   254	    polls: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, (u32, Option<i64>)>>,
   255	    clock: Arc<Mutex<crate::dev_clock::Clock>>,
   256	}
   257	
   258	impl Live {
   259	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#19.1 — presence remains server knowledge; development grace uses the same clock as the commit deciding effects.
   260	    fn new(clock: Arc<Mutex<crate::dev_clock::Clock>>) -> Self { Self { polls: Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new()), clock } }
   261	
   262	    fn enter(&self, principal: &str) {
   263	        let mut map = lock(&self.polls);
   264	        map.entry(principal.to_owned()).or_insert((0, None)).0 += 1;
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    fn leave(&self, principal: &str) {
   268	        let mut map = lock(&self.polls);
   269	        let now = lock(&self.clock).presence_now();
   270	        if let Some(entry) = map.get_mut(principal) {
   271	            entry.0 = entry.0.saturating_sub(1);
   272	            entry.1 = Some(now);
   273	        }
   274	        map.retain(|_, (open, last)| *open > 0 || last.is_some_and(|at| now.saturating_sub(at) < PRESENCE_GRACE.as_millis() as i64));
   275	    }
   420	fn dispatch_with_builder(request: Request, shared: Arc<Shared>, admission: Arc<RequestAdmission>, loopback_listener: bool, builder: std::thread::Builder) {
   421	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.7 — admission failures count; worker timing includes the listener wait.
   422	    let trace = trace::Trace::capture(&request, shared.personas && loopback_listener);
   423	    let sample = latency::Sample::with_headers(&request, &shared.latency, shared.personas && loopback_listener && dev_request(&request, &shared).is_ok());
   424	    let class = request_class(&request, &shared);
   425	    let Some(permit) = admission.acquire(class) else {
   426	        admission.refuse(request, class);
   427	        return;
   428	    };
   429	    let pending = PendingRequest { request: Some(request), admission: admission.clone(), class };
   430	    if let Err(error) = admission.workers[class as usize].submit(admission.limit(class), builder, Box::new(move || { let _permit = permit;
   431	        let request = pending.take();
   432	        let run = || { if let Some(sample) = sample { sample.run(|| handle(request, &shared)); } else { handle(request, &shared); } };
   433	        if let Some(trace) = trace { trace.run(run); } else { run(); } })) {
   434	        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: cannot start request thread: {error}");
   435	    }
   436	}

   438	fn detached_request(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
   439	    method == &Method::Post && (path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path == "/sync")
   440	        || method == &Method::Get && matches!(path, "/" | "/probe" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/dev/status" | "/dev/catalog" | "/auth/config")
   441	        || method == &Method::Options
   442	}
   443	
   444	fn is_waiter(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
   445	    (method == &Method::Get && path == "/changes") || (method == &Method::Post && path.starts_with("/a/"))
   446	}

   448	/// Service workers' claim polls: their own pool, so held viewer waits cannot
   449	/// starve effect and action delivery, nor the reverse.
   450	fn is_claim(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
   451	    method == &Method::Post && (path == "/effects/claim" || path == "/actions/claim")
   452	}

   454	/// `/changes`' own reading of `wait` (absent or unparsable is 0; capped at 25).
   455	pub(crate) fn wait_seconds(url: &str) -> u64 {
   456	    query_params(url).get("wait").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0).min(25)
   457	}

   459	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — stamp actual engine work after buffered intake, without pinning worker waits or socket I/O.
   460	pub(crate) fn producing_engine(shared:&Shared) -> engine_lock::FairGuard<'_,Engine> {
   461	    let engine=lock(&shared.engine);
   462	    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
   463	    engine
   464	}

   466	/// A worker's seat: a bearer session of a service principal.
   467	fn service_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
   468	    let engine=lock(&shared.engine);
   469	    let result=service_of_locked(request,shared,&engine);
   470	    if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
   471	    result
   472	}
   473	
   474	fn service_of_locked(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
   475	    dev_request(request, shared)?;
   476	    let refused = || Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "this is a worker's door: send a live service token (`snapback4 token --service <name>`)").guide("effects");
   477	    if !matches!(single_header(request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) { return Err(refused()); }
   478	    let token = crate::auth::bearer_token(request).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
   479	    snapback4_core::identity::current_service(engine.store(), &token, snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample {
   480	        now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire),
   481	    }).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?.ok_or_else(refused)
   482	}
   483	
   484	pub(crate) fn single_header<'a>(request: &'a Request, name: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>, ()> {
   485	    let mut headers = request.headers().iter().filter(|h| h.field.as_str().as_str().eq_ignore_ascii_case(name));
   486	    let value = headers.next().map(|h| h.value.as_str());
   487	    if headers.next().is_some() { Err(()) } else { Ok(value) }
   488	}

   510	enum SessionMintWire {
   511	    Legacy { principal: String, kind: String, days: i64 },
   512	    Bound { principal: String, kind: String, days: i64, binding: snapback4_core::identity::SessionMintBinding, lease: String, service_token: String },
   513	}
   514	
   515	fn default_session_kind() -> String { "account".into() }
   516	fn default_session_days() -> i64 { snapback4_core::identity::SESSION_DAYS }
   517	
   518	fn session_mint_wire(request: &Request, payload: &Json) -> Result<SessionMintWire, (u16, Refusal)> {
   519	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — service minting cannot silently widen a requested scope.
   520	    if payload.get("scope").is_some_and(|scope| scope != "full") {
   521	        return Err((400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "service minting accepts only full scope; narrow an eligible account session").guide("read-only")));
   522	    }
   523	    let has_lease = request.headers().iter().any(|header| header.field.as_str().as_str().eq_ignore_ascii_case("x-snapback-action-lease"));
   524	    let has_binding = payload.as_object().is_some_and(|object| object.contains_key("binding"));
   525	    if !has_lease && !has_binding {
   526	        let principal = payload.get("principal").and_then(Json::as_str).map(str::to_owned)
   527	            .ok_or_else(|| (400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "name the account: { \"principal\": \"member:42\" }")))?;
   528	        return Ok(SessionMintWire::Legacy {
   529	            principal,
   530	            kind: payload.get("kind").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or("account").to_owned(),
   531	            days: payload.get("days").and_then(Json::as_i64).unwrap_or(snapback4_core::identity::SESSION_DAYS),
   532	        });
   533	    }
   534	    let mut bound_payload = payload.clone();
   535	    if let Some(fields) = bound_payload.as_object_mut() { fields.remove("scope"); }
   536	    let parsed = serde_json::from_value::<BoundSessionWire>(bound_payload).map_err(|_| (400,

    16	fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    17	    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    18	    path.push("-token");
    19	    path.into()

    22	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
    23	pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    24	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    25	        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
    26	        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    27	        file.set_len(0)?;
    28	        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
    29	        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
    30	        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    31	    }
    32	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    33	        let _ = store;
    34	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    35	    }
    36	}
    37	
    38	fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    39	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    40	        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    41	        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    42	        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
    43	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
    44	        }
    45	        let mut secret = String::new();
    46	        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
    47	        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
    48	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(secret)
    51	    }
    52	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    53	        let _ = store;
    54	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    55	    }
    56	}

    59	    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
    60	}

    62	pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    63	    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
    64	        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
    65	        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
    66	}

    68	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
    69	pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    70	    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
    71	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
    72	            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
    73	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
    74	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    75	    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
    76	        return denied(request);
    77	    };
    78	    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
    79	        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    80	    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
    81	        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
    82	        _ => return denied(request),
    83	    };
    84	    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
    85	        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
    86	        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
    87	            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
    88	        },
    89	        _ => return denied(request),
    90	    };
    91	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    92	    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
    93	        return denied(request);
    94	    }
    95	    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
    96	        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
    97	        _ => return denied(request),
    98	    };
    99	    let binding = mint.binding();
   100	    let Some(server) = server else {
   101	        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
   102	        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
   103	            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
   104	    };
   105	    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
   106	        return denied(request);
   107	    }
   108	    let result = {
   109	        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
   110	        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
   111	        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
   112	        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
   113	            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, snapback4_core::now_ms(), mint.days)
   114	        })
   115	    };
   116	    let reply = match result {
   117	        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
   118	        Err(error) => {
   119	            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
   120	                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
   121	            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
   122	            json!({"denied": why})
   123	        }
   124	    };
   125	    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
   126	        return denied(request);
   127	    };
   128	    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
   129	}
   130	
   131	fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
   132	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
   133	    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
   134	    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
   135	    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
   136	    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
   137	    let mut response = String::new();
   138	    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
   139	    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
   140	        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
   141	        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
   142	        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
   143	}

   145	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
   146	pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
   147	    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
   148	    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
   149	    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
   150	    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
   151	    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
   152	    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
   153	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
   154	    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
   155	    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
   156	    let binding = mint.binding();
   157	    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
   158	    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
   159	    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
   160	    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
   161	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
   162	    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
   163	        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
   164	    }
   165	    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
   166	    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
   167	        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
   168	    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
   169	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
   170	    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
   171	        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
   172	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
   173	    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
   174	        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
   175	            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
   176	        }
   177	        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
   178	    }
   179	    let session = &reply["session"];
   180	    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
   181	    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
   182	    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
   183	        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
   184	    }
   185	    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
   186	}
