   333	
   334	// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
   335	// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
   336	struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
   337	impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
   338	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   339	        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
   340	        state.advancing = false;
   341	        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
   342	    }
   343	}
   344	
   345	fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   346	    if stopping {
   347	        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
   348	    }
   349	    Ok(work)
   350	}
   351	
   352	pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   353	    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
   354	    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
   355	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   356	        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
   357	    };
   358	    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
   359	        None => false,
   360	        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
   361	        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
   362	    };
   363	    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
   364	    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
   365	        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
   366	        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
   367	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   368	    };
   369	    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
   370	    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
   371	    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
   372	    let _advance = lock(&advance);
   373	    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
   374	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
   375	    let advanced = (|| {
   376	        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
   377	        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
   378	        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   379	        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
   380	        shared.dev_authority(&engine)?;
   381	        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
   382	        let mut next = clock.clone();
   383	        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
   384	        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules)?;
   385	        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
   386	        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
   387	        *clock = next;
   388	        let offset = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
   389	        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
   390	            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
   391	        } else { String::new() };
   392	        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (authoritative floor included); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
   393	        Ok((now, offset, message, before, boundaries))
   394	    })();
   395	    let (now, offset, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
   396	        Ok(result) => result,
   397	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   398	    };
   399	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
   400	    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
   401	    let drained = (|| {
   402	        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
   403	        if each_boundary {
   404	            for (index, at) in boundaries {
   405	                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(now, index, at))?);
   406	            }
   407	        }
   408	        let mut work = work(shared, true, 0)?;
   409	        work.schedules += crossed.len();
   410	        work.fired.extend(crossed);
   411	        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
   412	    })();
   413	    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
   414	        Ok(work) => {
   415	            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary);
   416	            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":offset, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
   417	        }
   418	        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
   419	        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
   420	        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":offset, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   421	    }
   422	}
   423	
   424	/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
   425	/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
   426	/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
   427	pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
   428	    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
   429	    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
   430	    let service=if service_route {
   431	        match super::session_identity::principal_of(&request,shared) {
   432	            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind=="service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Some(principal),
   433	            _ => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","triggering a deployed system job requires a full service token").guide("jobs"))})),
   434	        }
   435	    } else {
   436	        if !shared.personas {return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","/dev/jobs is a development door; use run <job> --release <dir> with a service token").guide("jobs"))}));}
   437	        None
   438	    };
   439	    let refuse = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| respond(request, status, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal.guide("jobs"))}));
   440	    let payload: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(if body.trim().is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
   441	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   442	        Err(error) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body is not JSON: {error}; send {{\"args\":{{..}}}}"))),
   443	    };
   444	    if service_route && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
   445	    let program = lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == name).map(|p| p.program.clone());
   446	    let program = match program {
   447	        Some(program) if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job => program,
   448	        Some(other) => return refuse(request, 404, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{name} is a {}, not a job; queries are POST /q/<op>, mutations POST /m/<op>", other.kind.word()))),
   449	        None => return respond(request, 404, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&super::dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown job {name}")).guide("jobs")))})),
   450	    };
   451	    if let Some(raw)=payload.get("args") && let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,raw) {return refuse(request,400,why);}
   452	    let mut args = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   453	    if let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) {
   454	        for (field, value) in fields {
   455	            let Some(kind) = program.args.get(field) else { return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{name} takes no argument {field}"))); };
   456	            match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
   457	                Ok(typed) => { args.insert(field.clone(), typed); }
   458	                Err(why) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {field}: {why}"))),
   459	            }
   460	        }
   461	    }
   462	    let mut admitted=lock(&shared.engine);
   463	    crate::release_requests::stamp(admitted.generation());
   464	    if !service_route && let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&admitted) { drop(admitted); return refuse(request,403,why); }
   465	    if let Some(service)=&service {
   466	        if let Err(why)=super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&admitted,service) {drop(admitted);return refuse(request,403,why);}
   467	    }
   468	    let queued=admitted.enqueue_job(name,args,shared.now());
   469	    drop(admitted);
   470	    crate::release_requests::executed();
   471	    let id = match queued {
   472	        Ok(id) => id,
   473	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   474	    };
   475	    // FIFO: earlier queued runs finish first, as with `run` on a stopped store;
   476	    // the background tick may run some chunks too, through the same engine.
   477	    let run = loop {
   478	        let current = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.job(&id)};
   479	        match current {
   480	            Ok(Some(run)) if matches!(run.state, JobState::Done | JobState::Parked) || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => break run,
   481	            Ok(Some(_)) => {}
   482	            Ok(None) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&RuntimeError::Store(format!("job {id} vanished")).refusal())})),
   483	            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   484	        }
   485	        let (chunk, logs) = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| (engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget), engine.take_native_logs()));
   486	        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
   487	        match chunk {
   488	            Ok(Some(chunk)) if chunk.state == JobState::Parked && shared.personas => shared.refusals.job(&chunk),
   489	            Ok(_) => {}
   490	            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   491	        }
   492	    };
   493	    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} {} on request: {} chunk{}, {} row{} written", run.name, if run.state == JobState::Done { "done" } else { "parked" },
   494	        run.chunks, if run.chunks == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, run.rows_written, if run.rows_written == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
   495	    respond(request, 200, json!({ "job": run.name, "id": run.id, "state": run.state, "chunks": run.chunks, "chunkSize": run.chunk_size, "retries": run.retries, "heapPeak": run.heap_peak, "rowsWritten": run.rows_written, "error": run.error }));
   496	}
   497	
   498	pub(super) fn clock_status(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
   499	    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
   500	    let result = (|| {
   501	        let (epoch, reader) = shared.reader()?;
   502	        let note = crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&reader, shared.now());
   503	        lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   504	        note
   505	    })();
   506	    match result {
   507	        Ok(note) => { let now = shared.now(); respond(request, 200, json!({"storeAhead":note,"now":now,"skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms()})) },
   508	        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   509	    }
   510	}

     5	/// Reuse only detached read owners. Each query still opens and rolls back

    22	impl QueryReaders {
    23	    pub(super) fn take(
    24	        &mut self,
    25	        engine: &Engine,
    26	    ) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    27	        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
    28	        if self.source.is_none() {
    29	            self.source = engine.reader().ok();
    30	        }
    31	        let reader = match self.idle.pop() {
    32	            Some(reader) => reader,
    33	            None => engine.reader()?,
    34	        };
    35	        Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader))
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    pub(super) fn put(&mut self, epoch: &Arc<()>, reader: Engine) {
    39	        if Arc::ptr_eq(epoch, &self.epoch) && self.idle.len() < REQUEST_WORKERS {
    40	            self.idle.push(reader);
    41	        }
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    pub(super) fn invalidate(&mut self) {
    45	        // Native code can change without a schema-generation change. Loans
    46	        // retain their old epoch so an in-flight return cannot repopulate it.
    47	        self.epoch = Arc::new(());
    48	        self.idle.clear();
    49	        self.source = None;
    50	        self.candidate_queries.clear();
    51	        self.launching_feed = false;
    52	    }
    53	}

    55	impl QueryReaders {
    56	    pub(super) fn new(engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) -> Self {
    57	        Self {
    58	            source: engine.reader().ok(),
    59	            refusal_sites: Arc::new(sites.clone()),
    60	            memory: engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty),
    61	            ..Self::default()
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	    pub(super) fn replace(&mut self, engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) {
    65	        self.invalidate();
    66	        self.refusal_sites = Arc::new(sites.clone());
    67	        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
    68	        self.source = engine.reader().ok();
    69	    }
    70	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — retry a failed factory on existing writer work; reads still refuse immediately.
    71	    pub(super) fn retry_missing(&mut self, engine: &Engine) {
    72	        if !self.memory && self.source.is_none() {
    73	            self.source = latency::acquire(|| {
    74	                #[cfg(test)]
    75	                if let Some(open) = &self.retry_open { open(); }
    76	                engine.reader()
    77	            }).ok();
    78	        }
    79	    }
    80	    pub(super) fn begin_adoption(&mut self, candidate: &Bound) {
    81	        self.launching_feed = self.source.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| candidate.schema.timelines.iter()
    82	            .any(|next| !old.schema().timelines.iter().any(|t| t.same_declaration(next))));
    83	        self.candidate_queries = candidate
    84	            .programs
    85	            .iter()
    86	            .filter(|p| p.program.kind == ProgramKind::Query)
    87	            .map(|p| p.program.name.clone())
    88	            .collect();
    89	    }
    90	    pub(super) fn finish_adoption(&mut self) {
    91	        self.candidate_queries.clear();
    92	        self.launching_feed = false;
    93	    }
    94	    pub(super) fn launching_feed(&self) -> bool { self.launching_feed }
    95	    pub(super) fn pending(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
    96	        self.candidate_queries.contains(name)
    97	    }
    98	    fn detached(&mut self) -> Option<Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError>> {
    99	        #[cfg(test)]
   100	        if let Some(remaining) = &mut self.fail_open_after {
   101	            if *remaining == 0 {
   102	                self.idle.clear();
   103	                return Some(Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("injected reader-open failure; retry the request".into())));
   104	            }
   105	            *remaining -= 1;
   106	        }
   107	        let source = self.source.as_ref()?;
   108	        Some(match self.idle.pop() {
   109	            Some(reader) => Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
   110	            None => source.reader().map(|reader| (self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
   111	        })
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	impl Shared {
   115	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — a fresh WAL snapshot reads the old program during atomic activation.
   116	    // The source is only a connection factory: no transaction, answer or viewer
   117	    // is retained. Identity guards still refuse an obsolete generation.
   118	    pub(super) fn reader(&self) -> Result<(Arc<()>, TimedReader), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   119	        let (result, sites) = self.acquire_reader(|| {
   120	            let start = trace::begin();
   121	            let mut readers = lock(&self.readers);
   122	            trace::waited(start, "readers");
   123	            (readers.detached(), readers.refusal_sites.clone())
   124	        });
   125	        if let Some(result) = result {
   126	            return result.map(|(epoch, reader)| (epoch, TimedReader { engine: reader, refusal_sites: sites, _timing: latency::EngineSpan::new() }));
   127	        }
   128	        // A missing factory can also mean opening a file-backed reader failed.
   129	        // Never acquire the writer to repair that failure under a read permit.
   130	        Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("detached reader unavailable; retry the request after the store is available".into()))
   131	    }
   132	    fn acquire_reader<T>(&self, open: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
   133	        latency::acquire(|| {
   134	            #[cfg(test)]
   135	            test_work(&self.reader_open);
   136	            open()
   137	        })
   138	    }
   139	    pub(super) fn open_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<Engine, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   140	        self.acquire_reader(|| engine.reader())
   141	    }
   142	    pub(super) fn take_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   143	        self.acquire_reader(|| lock(&self.readers).take(engine))
   144	    }
   145	    pub(super) fn memory_store(&self) -> bool { lock(&self.readers).memory }
   146	
   147	    // Identity, head and generation are captured from one reader incarnation.
   148	    pub(super) fn read_identity(&self) -> Result<(String, u64, u64), Refusal> {
   149	        match self.reader() {
   150	            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
   151	                let generation = reader.generation();
   152	                let head = response_head(&mut reader).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation));
   153	                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   154	                head
   155	            }
   156	            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => {
   157	                let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
   158	                let generation = engine.generation();
   159	                response_head(&mut engine).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation))
   160	            }
   161	            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
   162	        }
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    pub(super) fn read_head(&self) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
   166	        match self.reader() {
   167	            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
   168	                let head = response_head(&mut reader);
   169	                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   170	                head
   171	            }
   172	            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => response_head(&mut lock(&self.engine)),
   173	            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
   174	        }
   175	    }
   176	}

    36	fn unavailable_http_body_is_incomplete_for_legacy_clients_and_hides_release_meters() {
    37	    for dev in [true, false] {
    38	        let host = Host::with_store(dev, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),
    39	            "use identity\nquery costly():\n  text = ''\n  for n in range(0, 10000):\n    text = text + 'an allocation that stays bounded by the heap meter'\n  return [text]\n");
    40	        let session = body(&host.request("POST", "/auth/guest", "", "{}"));
    41	        let headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", session["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap());
    42	        let response = host.request("POST", "/q/costly", &headers, r#"{"args":{}}"#);
    43	        assert_eq!(status(&response), 200, "{response}");
    44	        let reply = body(&response);
    45	        assert_eq!(reply["loading"], true);
    46	        assert_eq!(reply["store_id"], lock(&host.shared.engine).store().meta("store_id").unwrap().unwrap());
    47	        assert!(reply.get("data").is_some_and(Json::is_null), "legacy data placeholder");
    48	        assert_eq!(reply["complete"], false, "legacy clients must not cache a complete undefined answer");
    49	        assert_eq!(reply.get("meter").is_some(), dev, "production omits the field entirely");
    50	    }
    51	}

    55	pub(super) struct Host {
    56	    server: Arc<Server>,
    57	    pub(super) shared: Arc<Shared>,
    58	    admission: Arc<RequestAdmission>,
    59	    worker: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
    60	    pub(super) address: SocketAddr,
    61	}
    62	impl Host {
    63	    fn new(dev: bool) -> Self {
    64	        Self::with_store(dev, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), SCHEMA)
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    pub(super) fn with_store(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str) -> Self {
    68	        Self::with_store_on(dev, store, source, "127.0.0.1")
    69	    }
    70	    fn with_store_on(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str, bind: &str) -> Self {
    71	        Self::with_evidence(dev, store, source, bind, None)
    72	    }
    73	    fn request(&self, method: &str, path: &str, headers: &str, body: &str) -> String {
    74	        let device = if path == "/sync" && !headers.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("x-snapback-device:") {
    75	            format!("x-snapback-device: {}\r\n", serde_json::to_string(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current()).unwrap())
    76	        } else { String::new() };
    77	        let headers = format!("{headers}{device}");
    78	        exchange(
    79	            self.address,
    80	            &format!(
    81	                "{method} {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n{headers}\r\n{body}",
    82	                body.len()
    83	            ),
    84	        )
    85	    }
    86	}
    87	impl Drop for Host {
    88	    fn drop(&mut self) {
    89	        *self.shared.seq.lock().unwrap() = u64::MAX;
    90	        lock(&self.shared.wake).seq=u64::MAX;
    91	        self.shared.committed.notify_all();
    92	        self.shared.polls.notify_all();
    93	        self.server.unblock();
    94	        self.worker.take().unwrap().join().unwrap();
    95	    }
    96	}
    97	
    98	pub(super) fn exchange(address: SocketAddr, wire: &str) -> String {
    99	    exchange_bytes(address, wire.as_bytes())
   100	}
   101	const CLIENT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3);
   102	const WAIT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);
   103	
   104	// @ref LLP 3000#publication-suite-under-contention — a parked client's guard
   105	// includes the driver's bounded waits/probes plus time to finish after release.
   106	// Only deliberately held exchanges use this; progress probes keep their guard.
   107	fn exchange_held(address: SocketAddr, hold_budget: Duration, wire: impl AsRef<[u8]>) -> String {
   108	    let wire = device_wire(wire.as_ref());
   109	    let timeout = hold_budget + Duration::from_secs(15);
   110	    assert!(timeout <= Duration::from_secs(60), "held exchange budget exceeds one minute");
   111	    let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
   112	    let remaining = || deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())
   113	        .filter(|left| !left.is_zero()).expect("held exchange deadline expired");
   114	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, remaining()).unwrap();
   115	    let mut wire = wire.as_slice();
   116	    while !wire.is_empty() {
   117	        stream.set_write_timeout(Some(remaining())).unwrap();
   118	        let written = stream.write(wire).unwrap();
   119	        assert!(written > 0, "held exchange write made no progress");
   120	        wire = &wire[written..];
   121	    }
   122	    let mut response = Vec::new();
   123	    let mut bytes = [0; 4096];
   124	    loop {
   125	        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(remaining())).unwrap();
   126	        let read = stream.read(&mut bytes).unwrap();
   127	        if read == 0 { break; }
   128	        response.extend_from_slice(&bytes[..read]);
   129	    }
   130	    String::from_utf8_lossy(&response).into_owned()
   131	}
   132	fn device_wire(wire: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
   133	    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(wire);
   134	    if text.starts_with("POST /sync ") && !text.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("x-snapback-device:") {
   135	        let at = text.find("\r\n").unwrap() + 2;
   136	        let header = format!("x-snapback-device: {}\r\n", serde_json::to_string(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current()).unwrap());
   137	        [wire[..at].to_vec(), header.into_bytes(), wire[at..].to_vec()].concat()
   138	    } else { wire.to_vec() }
   139	}
   140	fn exchange_bytes(address: SocketAddr, wire: &[u8]) -> String {
   141	    let wire = device_wire(wire);
   142	    let wire = wire.as_slice();
   143	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(address).unwrap();
   144	    stream
   145	        .set_read_timeout(Some(CLIENT_TIMEOUT))
   146	        .unwrap();
   147	    stream.write_all(wire).unwrap();
   148	    let mut response = Vec::new();
   149	    stream.read_to_end(&mut response).unwrap();
   150	    String::from_utf8_lossy(&response).into_owned()
   151	}
   152	pub(super) fn status(response: &str) -> u16 {
   153	    response.split(' ').nth(1).unwrap().parse().unwrap()
   154	}
   155	pub(super) fn body(response: &str) -> Json {
   156	    serde_json::from_str(response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").unwrap().1).unwrap()
   157	}
   158	fn effect_snapshot(host: &Host, id: &str) -> (Vec<rusqlite::types::Value>, i64) {
   159	    let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
   160	    let mut statement = engine.store().conn().prepare("SELECT * FROM _outbox WHERE id = ?1").unwrap();
   161	    let columns = statement.column_count();
   162	    let row = statement.query_row([id], |row| (0..columns).map(|column| row.get(column)).collect()).unwrap();
   163	    let steps = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _effect_steps WHERE effect_id = ?1", [id], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
   164	    (row, steps)
   165	}
   166	fn wait_until(mut predicate: impl FnMut() -> bool) {
   167	    let deadline = Instant::now() + WAIT_TIMEOUT;
   168	    while !predicate() {
   169	        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "condition did not become true");
   170	        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
   171	    }
   172	}

   174	#[test]
   175	fn raced_mutation_id_refusal_keeps_its_terminal_http_status() {
   176	    // Exercise the post-preflight error classifier directly: a socket race
   177	    // may otherwise happen to be caught by the earlier replay check.
   178	    assert_eq!(mutation_error_status(&snapback4_core::store::id_reused().refusal()), 200);
   179	    let mut transient = Refusal::new("E_STORE", "store", "retry later");
   180	    transient.retryable = true;
   181	    assert_eq!(mutation_error_status(&transient), 503);
   182	    assert_eq!(mutation_error_status(&Refusal::new("E_STORE", "store", "failed")), 500);
   183	
   184	    let host = Host::with_store(true, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),
   185	        "use personas alice\nmutation answer(n: int):\n  return n\n");
   186	    let gate = Arc::new(std::sync::Barrier::new(13));
   187	    let threads: Vec<_> = (0..12).map(|n| {
   188	        let gate = gate.clone();
   189	        let address = host.address;
   190	        thread::spawn(move || {
   191	            let payload = json!({ "id": "raced-input", "args": { "n": n } }).to_string();
   192	            gate.wait();
   193	            exchange(address, &format!("POST /m/answer HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len()))
   194	        })
   195	    }).collect();
   196	    gate.wait();
   197	    let replies: Vec<_> = threads.into_iter().map(|thread| {
   198	        let response = thread.join().unwrap();
   199	        assert_eq!(status(&response), 200, "{response}");
   200	        body(&response)
   201	    }).collect();
   202	    assert_eq!(replies.iter().filter(|reply| reply["state"] == "sent").count(), 1);
   203	    for reply in replies.iter().filter(|reply| reply["state"] != "sent") {
   204	        assert_eq!(reply["state"], "failed");
   205	        assert_eq!(reply["why"]["code"], "E_ID_REUSED");
   206	        assert!(!reply["why"]["retryable"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false));
   207	    }
   208	}

    30	/// Whether `POST /assets` carries `as=file`.
    31	pub fn is_file_upload(url: &str) -> bool {
    32	    crate::serve::query_params(url).get("as").is_some_and(|kind| kind == "file")
    33	}

    35	/// The most bytes a file upload may stream: the schema's largest file
    36	/// bound, under the general upload ceiling. A schema with no file column
    37	/// refuses before any body is read.
    38	pub fn file_ceiling(shared: &Shared) -> Result<usize, Refusal> {
    39	    match producing_engine(shared).file_upload_ceiling() {
    40	        Some(max) => Ok((max as usize).min(UPLOAD_CEILING)),
    41	        None => Err(Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "no column of this backend takes a file; declare one (`doc: file <=10000000`)").guide("assets")),
    42	    }
    43	}
    44	
    45	pub fn upload(request: Request, body: Vec<u8>, shared: &Shared) {
    46	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    47	        Ok(principal) => principal.0,
    48	        Err(refusal) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
    49	    };
    50	    if body.is_empty() {
    51	        return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "the upload is empty: POST the file's bytes as the body").guide("assets")) }));
    52	    }
    53	    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    54	    let file = match params.get("as").map(String::as_str) {
    55	        None | Some("image" | "video") => None,
    56	        Some("file") => Some(params.get("name").cloned().unwrap_or_default()),
    57	        Some(other) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("as={other} is not an upload kind; use as=file for a document, or omit it for an image or video")).guide("assets")) })),
    58	    };
    59	    let declared = match crate::serve::single_header(&request, "content-type") {
    60	        Ok(value) => snapback4_core::assets::declared_type(value),
    61	        Err(()) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "send one Content-Type header").guide("assets")) })),
    62	    };
    63	    let intent = params.get("intent").map(String::as_str);
    64	    if intent.is_some_and(|id| id.is_empty() || id.len() > 128 || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')) {
    65	        return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "intent is 1–128 ASCII letters, digits, hyphens or underscores")}));
    66	    }
    67	    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    68	    let fingerprint = snapback4_core::value::digest(&(format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&body)), &file.as_ref().map(|name| snapback4_core::assets::sanitize_name(name)), &declared));
    69	    let expected_store = params.get("store_id");
    70	    if intent.is_some() && expected_store.is_none() { return respond(request,400,json!({"denied":Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","intent uploads require store_id from the identified backend")})); }
    71	    let stored = (|| {
    72	        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
    73	            if producing_engine(shared).store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) {
    74	                return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_STORE_REPLACED", message: "upload intent belongs to another backend store".into() });
    75	            }
    76	        }
    77	        if let Some(id) = intent {
    78	            if let Some(asset) = producing_engine(shared).asset_intent(&principal, id, &fingerprint)? { return Ok(asset); }
    79	        }
    80	        let placement=if let Some(id)=intent {Some(producing_engine(shared).asset_declaration(&principal,id)?.ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_INTENT",message:"declare this intent's immutable placement before sending its bytes".into()} )?)}else{None};
    81	        let upload = match &file {
    82	            // @ref LLP 3000.038#2 — the declared type is verified against the bytes before admission.
    83	            Some(name) => {
    84	                let content_type = snapback4_core::assets::verify_file(&body, declared.as_deref())?;
    85	                producing_engine(shared).prepare_file(&principal, content_type, name, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
    86	            }
    87	            None => {
    88	                let sniffed = snapback4_core::assets::sniff(&body)?;
    89	                producing_engine(shared).prepare_asset(&principal, sniffed, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
    90	            }
    91	        };
    92	        #[cfg(test)] crate::serve::test_work(&shared.upload_work);
    93	        let upload = if let Some(id) = intent { upload.with_intent(id, &fingerprint).with_placement(placement.unwrap()) } else { upload };
    94	        let upload = upload.write(&body)?;
    95	        // Logout while disk I/O was in flight cannot publish a new asset.
    96	        principal_of(&request, shared).map_err(|_| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth("sign in again".into()))?;
    97	        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
    98	        crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    99	        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
   100	            if engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"upload store changed during disk write".into()}); }
   101	        }
   102	        engine.finish_asset(upload)
   103	    })();
   104	    match stored {
   105	        Ok(asset) => {
   106	            let mut facts = json!({ "id": asset.id, "type": asset.content_type, "bytes": asset.bytes, "width": asset.width, "height": asset.height, "duration": asset.duration });
   107	            if let Some(name) = asset.name { facts["name"] = json!(name); }
   108	            respond(request, 200, json!({ "asset": facts }))
   109	        }
   110	        Err(error) => {
   111	            let refusal = error.refusal();
   112	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{} at POST /assets: {}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
   113	            respond(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }))
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	}
   117	
   118	pub fn serve(request: Request, id: &str, shared: &Shared) {
   119	    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
   120	        Ok(capability) => capability,
   121	        Err(refusal) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
   122	    };
   123	    if id.is_empty() || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
   124	        return respond(request, 404, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_UNKNOWN", "asset", "no asset is served under that id to this viewer").guide("assets")) }));
   125	    }
   126	    let _transfer = shared.asset_transfers.enter(id, &principal);
   127	    let range_requested = request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("range"));
   128	    let granted = {
   129	        let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
   130	        let _persona_authority = match crate::serve::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
   131	            Ok(authority) => authority,
   132	            Err(why) => {
   133	            drop(engine); return respond(request, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
   134	            }
   135	        };
   136	        crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
   137	        engine.asset_access_request(&principal, id, shared.now(), range_requested)
   138	    };
   139	    let grant = match granted {
   140	        Ok(grant) => grant,
   141	        Err(error) => {
   142	            let refusal = error.refusal();
   143	            let status = match refusal.code.as_str() {
   144	                "E_ASSET_VIEWED" => 410,
   145	                "E_ASSET_RANGE" => 416,
   146	                "E_ASSET_UNKNOWN" => 404,
   147	                _ if refusal.retryable => 503,
   148	                _ => 500,
   149	            };
   150	            return respond(request, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));

     1	//! Request-side generation discovery; row-page wire forms remain in serve.rs.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use snapback4_core::error::RuntimeError;

     5	enum SyncOutcome<T> {
     6	    Generation(Json),
     7	    Version(Json),
     8	    Page(T),
     9	}
    10	
    11	#[cfg(test)]
    12	pub(super) static MATERIALIZATIONS: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, usize>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());
    13	
    14	// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#5 — learn the answering generation before materializing a stale page.
    15	fn under_generation<T>(engine: &mut Engine, requested: Option<u64>, generation: u64, store_id: &str, device: &snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine,
    16	    materialize: impl FnOnce(&mut Engine) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>,
    17	) -> Result<SyncOutcome<T>, RuntimeError> {
    18	    if let Err(denied) = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(device), &snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&engine.schema().public_projection())) {
    19	        return Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) })));
    20	    }
    21	    if requested.is_some_and(|requested| requested != generation) {
    22	        let denied = Refusal::new("E_SYNC_GENERATION", "generation", "sync generation changed; adopt /schema before retrying this position").guide("sync");
    23	        // Same head as a caught-up page: MAX(_commits.seq), not a delivered cut.
    24	        let watermark = engine.seq()?;
    25	        let reply = json!({
    26	            "denied": refusal_json(&denied), "generation": generation,
    27	            "store_id": store_id, "watermark": watermark,
    28	        });
    29	        return Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply));
    30	    }
    31	    let page = materialize(engine)?;
    32	    #[cfg(test)]
    33	    { *lock(&MATERIALIZATIONS).entry(store_id.to_owned()).or_default() += 1; }
    34	    Ok(SyncOutcome::Page(page))
    35	}
    36	
    37	pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str, now: i64) {
    38	    // This is also the page path's request authorization. Partition rules
    39	    // filter unreadable rows/groups; they add no whole-request refusal.
    40	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    41	        Ok(principal) => principal.0,
    42	        Err(refusal) => {
    43	            respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
    44	            return;
    45	        }
    46	    };
    47	    // The header envelope is parsed before SyncRequest, so an old wire shape
    48	    // cannot fall through to a missing-field or schema-decoder refusal.
    49	    if let Err(denied) = crate::device_handshake::check(shared, crate::device_handshake::header(&request), true) {
    50	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 409, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) }));
    51	    }
    52	    let device: snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine = serde_json::from_str(crate::device_handshake::header(&request).expect("checked handshake")).expect("checked handshake");
    53	    let input = match serde_json::from_str::<SyncRequest>(body) {
    54	        Ok(input) if input.pending.len() <= 256 && input.pending.iter().all(|id| !id.is_empty() && id.len() <= 128) =>
    55	        {
    56	            input
    57	        }
    58	        _ => {
    59	            return respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "sync needs { generation?, from, limit, after, stream, pending }: nonnegative integers, a cursor or null, a boolean, and at most 256 ids of 1..128 UTF-8 bytes")) }));
    60	        }
    61	    };
    62	    // @ref LLP 3000.026#4-atomic-launch-adoption — sync has its own bounded
    63	    // admission pool and authenticates before waiting on atomic launch.
    64	    let launching = lock(&shared.readers).launching_feed();
    65	    if launching {
    66	        let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    67	        // A long wait cannot preserve an expired or revoked bearer.
    68	        let authority = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal);
    69	        drop(engine);
    70	        if let Err(refusal) = authority {
    71	            respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
    72	            return;
    73	        }
    74	    }
    75	    let SyncRequest { generation: requested_generation, from, limit, mut after, stream, pending, store_id: requested_store } = input;
    76	    let store_id = match shared.read_head() {
    77	        Ok((id, _)) => id,
    78	        Err(error) => {
    79	            return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) }));
    80	        }
    81	    };
    82	    // An old snapshot cursor belongs to its database, not merely its schema.
    83	    if requested_store.as_ref().is_some_and(|id| id != &store_id) { after = None; }
    84	    let limit = limit.clamp(1, 10_000);
    85	    if from == 0 && !stream {
    86	        // `after` is the previous snapshot page's opaque `next` token.
    87	        let (outcome, generation) = {
    88	            let reader = shared.reader().map(|(_,reader)|reader);
    89	            match reader {
    90	                Ok(mut reader) => {
    91	                    let _persona_authority = match super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &reader) {
    92	                        Ok(authority) => authority,
    93	                        Err(why) => {
    94	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
    95	                        }
    96	                    };
    97	                    let generation = reader.generation();
    98	                    (under_generation(&mut reader, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |reader| {
    99	                                reader.snapshot_acquisition_page_at(&principal, after.as_deref(), limit, now).and_then(|page| {
   100	                                        reader.acknowledged(&principal, &pending, page.watermark).map(|acks| (page, acks))
   101	                                    })
   102	                            },
   103	                        ), generation,
   104	                    )
   105	                }
   106	                Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
   107	                    return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
   108	                }
   109	                Err(_) => {
   110	                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   111	                    let _persona_authority = match super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
   112	                        Ok(authority) => authority,
   113	                        Err(why) => {
   114	                        drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
   115	                        }
   116	                    };
   117	                    let generation = engine.generation();
   118	                    (under_generation(&mut engine, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |engine| {
   119	                                engine.snapshot_acquisition_page_at(&principal, after.as_deref(), limit, now).and_then(|page| {
   120	                                        engine.acknowledged(&principal, &pending, page.watermark).map(|acks| (page, acks))
   121	                                    })
   122	                            },
   123	                        ), generation,
   124	                    )
   125	                }
   126	            }
   127	        };
   128	        if shared.memory_store() {
   129	            shared.publish_engine(&lock(&shared.engine));
   130	        }
   131	        match outcome {
   132	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Page((page, acknowledged))) => {
   133	                let next = page.next.map(|next| next.encode());
   134	                let (events, watermark, held) = (page.events, page.watermark, page.held);
   135	                let overflow = json!({ "generation": generation, "store_id": store_id, "watermark": watermark });
   136	                respond_with_sync(request, 200, SnapshotReply {
   137	                    sent: acknowledged.clone(),
   138	                    acquisitions: page.acquisitions, acknowledged, events: SyncEvents::Snapshot(events), generation, store_id,
   139	                    held, more: next.is_some(), next, after, now, snapshot: true, watermark,
   140	                }, true, Some(overflow), Some(shared));
   141	            }
   142	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 409, reply),
   143	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply),
   144	            Err(error) => { let refusal = error.refusal(); respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if refusal.code == "E_INPUT" { 400 } else { 500 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })) }
   145	        }
   146	    } else {
   147	        let (outcome, generation) = {
   148	            let reader = shared.reader().map(|(_,reader)|reader);
   149	            match reader {
   150	                Ok(mut reader) => {
   151	                    let _persona_authority = match super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &reader) {
   152	                        Ok(authority) => authority,
   153	                        Err(why) => {
   154	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
   155	                        }
   156	                    };
   157	                    let generation = reader.generation();
   158	                    (under_generation(&mut reader, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |reader| {
   159	                                reader.stream_at(&principal, from, limit, now).and_then(|page| {
   160	                                        reader.acknowledged_at(&principal, &pending, page.watermark, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)).map(|acks| { let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(reader.store().conn(), reader.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); let changes = super::changes::settlement_changes(reader.store().conn(), reader.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); (page, acks, preserved, changes) })
   161	                                    })
   162	                            },
   163	                        ), generation,
   164	                    )
   165	                }
   166	                Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
   167	                    return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
   168	                }
   169	                Err(_) => {
   170	                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   171	                    let _persona_authority = match super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
   172	                        Ok(authority) => authority,
   173	                        Err(why) => {
   174	                        drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
   175	                        }
   176	                    };
   177	                    let generation = engine.generation();
   178	                    (under_generation(&mut engine, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |engine| {
   179	                                engine.stream_at(&principal, from, limit, now).and_then(|page| {
   180	                                        engine.acknowledged_at(&principal, &pending, page.watermark, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)).map(|acks| { let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); let changes = super::changes::settlement_changes(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); (page, acks, preserved, changes) })
   181	                                    })
   182	                            },
   183	                        ), generation,
   184	                    )
   185	                }
   186	            }
   187	        };
   188	        if shared.memory_store() {
   189	            shared.publish_engine(&lock(&shared.engine));
   190	        }
   191	        match outcome {
   192	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Page((page, (acknowledged, committed_above), preserved, changes))) => {
   193	                let mut overflow = json!({ "generation": generation, "store_id": store_id, "watermark": page.watermark });
   194	                // Whole commits cannot be split by lowering the stream limit.
   195	                // A snapshot pages their current rows individually instead.
   196	                if limit == 1 { overflow["snapshot"] = json!(true); }
   197	                respond_with_sync(request, 200, StreamReply {
   198	                    preserved, changes,
   199	                    sent: acknowledged.iter().chain(committed_above.iter()).map(|(id, seq)| (id.clone(), *seq)).collect(),
   200	                    images_at: page.images_at, committed_above, acquisitions: page.acquisitions, acknowledged, events: SyncEvents::Stream(page.events), generation, store_id,
   201	                    joined: page.joined, more: page.more, now, snapshot: false, watermark: page.watermark,
   202	                }, true, Some(overflow), Some(shared));
   203	            }
   204	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 409, reply),
   205	            Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply),
   206	            Err(RuntimeError::Bound { .. }) => {
   207	                let refusal = Refusal::new("E_BOUND", "bound", "the HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB; request a smaller page or return fewer values").guide("reads");
   208	                let mut reply = json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal), "generation": generation,
   209	                    "store_id": store_id, "watermark": from });
   210	                if limit == 1 { reply["snapshot"] = json!(true); }

     1	//! Keep a refused dev adoption pending until the writer can adopt it.
     2	use super::{Shared, lock};

     4	use snapback4_core::RuntimeError;

     7	fn locked(error: &RuntimeError) -> bool {
     8	    matches!(error,
     9	        RuntimeError::Sql(sql) if matches!(sql.sqlite_error_code(), Some(rusqlite::ErrorCode::DatabaseBusy | rusqlite::ErrorCode::DatabaseLocked)))
    10	}
    11	
    12	fn retryable(error: &RuntimeError) -> bool { locked(error) || matches!(error, RuntimeError::Generation(_)) }

    14	pub(super) fn open(owner: &crate::store_owner::Owner, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound, clock: &crate::dev_clock::Clock) -> Result<snapback4_core::Engine, RuntimeError> {
    15	    let mut reported = None;
    16	    loop {
    17	        match owner.open_engine(bound, Duration::from_secs(5), clock.now()) {
    18	            Ok(engine) => return Ok(engine),
    19	            Err(error) if locked(&error) && !crate::lifecycle::stopping() => {
    20	                let reason = error.to_string();
    21	                if reported.as_ref() != Some(&reason) {
    22	                    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: startup schema adoption failed: {reason}; no requests, jobs or sweeps are running; retrying automatically");
    23	                    reported = Some(reason);
    24	                }
    25	                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400));
    26	            }
    27	            Err(error) => return Err(error),
    28	        }
    29	    }
    30	}

    32	const BACKGROUND_WINDOW: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
    33	const BACKGROUND_BURST: u8 = 2;
    34	
    35	struct Background {
    36	    message: String,
    37	    window: Instant,
    38	    emitted: u8,
    39	}
    40	
    41	pub(super) struct Status {
    42	    pub store_id: String,
    43	    pub failure: Option<String>,
    44	    background: [Option<Background>; 2],
    45	    suppressed: u64,
    46	    retrying: bool,
    47	}
    48	impl Status {
    49	    pub fn new(store_id: String) -> Self { Self { store_id, failure: None, background: [None, None], suppressed: 0, retrying: false } }
    50	    fn recovery(&self) -> &str { if self.retrying { "retrying automatically" } else { "fix the schema and save again" } }
    51	    pub(super) fn refused(&mut self, error: &RuntimeError, generation: u64) {
    52	        let reason = error.to_string();
    53	        self.retrying = retryable(error);
    54	        if self.failure.as_ref() != Some(&reason) {
    55	            if let Some(line) = self.reset_background("adoption failure changed") { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}"); }
    56	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: the new schema was not adopted: {reason}; serving generation {generation}; jobs and sweeps may fail until adoption succeeds; {}", self.recovery());
    57	            self.failure = Some(reason);
    58	        }
    59	    }
    60	    pub(super) fn background_failure(&mut self, name: &str, error: &RuntimeError) -> Option<String> {
    61	        self.background_failure_at(name, error, Instant::now())
    62	    }
    63	    pub(super) fn background_failure_at(&mut self, name: &str, error: &RuntimeError, now: Instant) -> Option<String> {
    64	        let slot = match error.code().as_str() { "E_STORE" => Some(0), "E_GENERATION" => Some(1), _ => None };
    65	        if let Some(slot) = slot && let Some(reason) = &self.failure {
    66	            // @ref LLP 3000#7 — keep adoption consequences visible without flooding background logs.
    67	            // Two changed reasons per code per minute; identical repeats stay quiet
    68	            // even after the window expires. Suppressed calls never extend the window.
    69	            let error = error.to_string();
    70	            if let Some(background) = &mut self.background[slot] {
    71	                if background.message == error || (now.duration_since(background.window) < BACKGROUND_WINDOW && background.emitted >= BACKGROUND_BURST) {
    72	                    self.suppressed = self.suppressed.saturating_add(1);
    73	                    return None;
    74	                }
    75	                if now.duration_since(background.window) >= BACKGROUND_WINDOW {
    76	                    background.window = now;
    77	                    background.emitted = 0;
    78	                }
    79	                background.message = error.clone();
    80	                background.emitted += 1;
    81	            } else {
    82	                self.background[slot] = Some(Background { message: error.clone(), window: now, emitted: 1 });
    83	            }
    84	            let line = format!("snapback4: {name} failed: {error}; adoption still pending: {reason}; jobs and sweeps may fail until adoption succeeds; {}", self.recovery());
    85	            Some(line)
    86	        } else { Some(format!("snapback4: {name} failed: {error}")) }
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    pub(super) fn recovered(&mut self) -> Option<String> {
    90	        self.failure = None;
    91	        self.retrying = false;
    92	        self.reset_background("adoption recovered")
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    fn reset_background(&mut self, reason: &str) -> Option<String> {
    96	        self.background = [None, None];
    97	        let count = std::mem::take(&mut self.suppressed);
    98	        (count > 0).then(|| format!("snapback4 dev: {reason}; suppressed {count} repeated background failures"))
    99	    }
   100	}
   101	
   102	pub(super) fn background_failure(shared: &Shared, name: &str, error: &RuntimeError) {
   103	    if let Some(line) = lock(&shared.adoption).background_failure(name, error) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}"); }
   104	}

   106	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — every rejected candidate retires its pending query names.
   107	pub(super) fn adopt_candidate(
   108	    shared: &Shared,
   109	    engine: &mut snapback4_core::Engine,
   110	    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
   111	    reopen: impl FnOnce() -> Result<snapback4_core::Engine, RuntimeError>,
   112	) -> Result<(u64, bool), RuntimeError> {
   113	    lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(bound);
   114	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.3 — retries judge the persisted successor, retaining the generation lock.
   115	    let adopted = engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), shared.now());
   116	    let adopted = match adopted {
   117	        Err(RuntimeError::Generation(_)) => reopen().and_then(|mut next| {
   118	            next.share_ephemeral_hub(shared.ephemeral.clone())?;
   119	            next.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
   120	            *engine = next;
   121	            Ok((engine.generation(), true))
   122	        }),
   123	        other => other.map(|generation| (generation, false)),
   124	    };
   125	    if let Err(error) = &adopted {
   126	        lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
   127	        lock(&shared.adoption).refused(error, engine.generation());
   128	    } else if let Some(line) = lock(&shared.adoption).recovered() {
   129	        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}");
   130	    }
   131	    adopted
   132	}

   134	pub(super) fn watch_sources(project: Project, shared: Arc<Shared>, mut stamp: crate::project::SourceStamp, verbose: bool) {
   135	    let mut retry = false;
   136	    let mut seen_generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
   137	    loop {
   138	        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400));
   139	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   140	        let now = project.sources_stamp();
   141	        let stored_generation = {
   142	            let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   143	            engine.store().meta("generation").ok().flatten().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(seen_generation)
   144	        };
   145	        if now == stamp && !retry && stored_generation == seen_generation {
   146	            continue;
   147	        }
   148	        stamp = now;
   149	        seen_generation = stored_generation;
   150	        retry = false;
   151	        let started = Instant::now();
   152	        match project.compile() {
   153	            Ok(bound) if bound.diagnostics.is_empty() => {
   154	                let rebuilt = match crate::native::build(&project, &bound) {
   155	                    Ok(built) => built,
   156	                    Err(problem) => {
   157	                        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: {problem}\nsnapback4 dev: serving the previous generation");
   158	                        continue;
   159	                    }
   160	                };
   161	                let residency_notes = snapback4_lang::bind::residency::change_notes(&bound, &lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations);
   162	                let steps = snapback4_core::successor::classify(&lock(&shared.bound).schema, &bound.schema);
   163	                let worst = snapback4_core::successor::worst(&steps);
   164	                let generated = match project.write_generated(&bound) {
   165	                    Ok(generated) => generated,
   166	                    Err(error) => {
   167	                        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: cannot write generated types: {error}\nsnapback4 dev: serving the previous generation");
   168	                        continue;
   169	                    }
   170	                };
   171	                if let Err(error) = project.check_server_at(&mut stamp) {
   172	                    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: {error}\nsnapback4 dev: serving the previous generation");
   173	                    continue;
   174	                }
   175	                let declared = match crate::dev_worker::declared(&project) {
   176	                    Ok(workers) => workers,
   177	                    Err(error) => {
   178	                        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: {error}\nsnapback4 dev: serving the previous generation");
   179	                        continue;
   180	                    }
   181	                };
   182	                if crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() || !declared.is_empty() {
   183	                    if let Err(error) = crate::dev_worker::validate_environment(&bound.schema) {
   184	                        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: {error}\nsnapback4 dev: serving the previous generation");
   185	                        continue;
   186	                    }
   187	                }
   188	                let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   189	                let adopted = adopt_candidate(&shared, &mut engine, &bound, || {
   190	                    shared.owner.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("temporary server cannot reopen a persisted store".into()))
   191	                        .and_then(|owner| owner.open_engine(&bound, Duration::from_secs(5), shared.now()))
   192	                });
   193	                match adopted {
   194	                    Ok((next, reopened)) => {
   195	                        shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
   196	                        seen_generation = next;
   197	                        crate::lifecycle::generation(next);
   198	
   199	                        if reopened || rebuilt.is_some() {
   200	                            match crate::native::install(&mut engine, &project) {
   201	                                Ok(installed) => {
   202	                                    *lock(&shared.native) = installed;
   203	                                    if let Some(built) = &rebuilt { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: native module rebuilt ({} B, {} ms): {}", built.bytes, built.ms, built.jobs.join(", ")); }
   204	                                }
   205	                                Err(problem) => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: {problem}"),
   206	                            }
   207	                        }
   208	                        lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
   209	                        // Publish the advertised backend and identity together:
   210	                        // /schema takes these guards in the same order.
   211	                        let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
   212	                        let mut generation = lock(&shared.generation);
   213	                        for note in &residency_notes { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("note: {note}"); }
   214	                        for note in &bound.notes { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("note: {note}"); }
   215	                        shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
   216	                        *active = bound;
   217	                        *generation = next;
   218	                        // Claims authenticate their supervisor revision while
   219	                        // holding this engine guard: the old child cannot
   220	                        // claim new work during the supervisor's stop delay.

  1096	fn dev_loopback_admits_eight_simultaneous_sign_ins_within_host_capacity() {
  1097	    let host = Host::new(true);
  1098	    let payload = r#"{"email":"parallel@example.com","password":"correct horse battery staple"}"#;
  1099	    let signup = body(&host.request("POST", "/auth/signup", "", payload));
  1100	    assert!(signup["session"]["token"].is_string(), "{signup}");
  1101	    let gate = Arc::new(WorkGate::default());
  1102	    let _release_on_panic = ReleaseGate(gate.clone());
  1103	    let hook = gate.clone();
  1104	    *host.shared.auth_work.lock().unwrap() = Some(Arc::new(move || hook.park()));
  1105	    // Smaller hosts must still respect their physical limit; this host runs
  1106	    // eight at once whenever available_parallelism supplies at least eight.
  1107	    let concurrent = thread::available_parallelism().map(|n| n.get().clamp(2, 8)).unwrap_or(2);
  1108	    let jobs: Vec<_> = (0..concurrent).map(|_| {
  1109	        let address = host.address;
  1110	        thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, WAIT_TIMEOUT + CLIENT_TIMEOUT, &format!("POST /auth/login HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len())))
  1111	    }).collect();
  1112	    wait_until(|| gate.entered.load(Ordering::Acquire) == concurrent);
  1113	    let query = host.request("POST", "/q/who", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", "{}");
  1114	    assert_eq!(body(&query)["data"], "dev:alice");
  1115	    gate.release();
  1116	    for job in jobs {
  1117	        let response = job.join().unwrap();
  1118	        let result = body(&response);
  1119	        assert_eq!(status(&response), 200, "{response}");
  1120	        assert!(result["session"]["token"].is_string(), "{response}");
  1121	        assert_eq!(result["session"]["principal"], signup["session"]["principal"]);
  1122	        assert!(result.get("denied").is_none(), "{response}");
  1123	    }
  1124	}

     9	const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
    10	process.once("exit", () => { for (const child of children) child.kill("SIGKILL"); });
    11	export function register(child: ChildProcess): void {
    12	  children.add(child);
    13	  child.once("exit", () => children.delete(child));
    14	  child.on("error", () => children.delete(child));

    19	  args?: readonly string[];
    20	  timeoutMs?: number;
    21	  stopTimeoutMs?: number;
    22	  logsDir?: string;
    23	  /** Keep stdout in memory only (stderr remains diagnostic evidence). */
    24	  secret?: boolean;
    25	  /** Internal: a frontend keeper owns this private process group. */
    26	  detached?: boolean;
    27	  /** Internal: the frontend observes parent death through stdin EOF. */
    28	  livenessPipe?: boolean;
    29	}
    30	export interface ProofHost {
    31	  url: string;
    32	  pid: number;
    33	  logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string };
    34	  stop(): Promise<void>;
    35	}
    36	export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
    37	  const value = ms ?? fallback;
    38	  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");
    39	  return value;
    40	}
    41	export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
    42	  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    43	  try {
    44	    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    45	      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    46	    })]);
    47	  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    48	}
    49	/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
    50	export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
    51	  const errors: unknown[] = [];
    52	  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    53	    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    54	    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
    55	  }
    56	  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
    57	}
    58	export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
    59	  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
    60	  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    61	    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 5000, "host stop"); return;
    62	  }
    63	  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
    64	      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
    65	      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    66	    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
    67	  }
    68	  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
    69	  let done!: () => void;
    70	  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
    71	  child.once("exit", done);
    72	  child.once("error", done);
    73	  try {
    74	    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    75	    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    76	    catch {
    77	      child.kill("SIGKILL");
    78	      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    79	    }
    80	  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
    81	}
    82	export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
    83	  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
    84	  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
    85	  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)
    86	      || Number(low) < 1 || Number(high) > 65535 || Number(low) > Number(high)) {
    87	    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
    88	  }
    89	  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
    90	}
    91	/** App-local evidence survives harness cleanup of its temporary root. */
    92	export function logsDir(options: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> = {}): string {
    93	  return resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", options.logsDir ?? "logs");
    94	}
    95	export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
    96	  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
    97	  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    98	  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
    99	  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
   100	  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
   101	  let child: ChildProcess;
   102	  try { child = spawn(command, [...args], { cwd, detached: options.detached ?? false, env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err] }); }
   103	  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
   104	  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
   105	  let stdout = "";
   106	  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
   107	  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
   108	  register(child);
   109	  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
   110	  let error: Error | undefined;
   111	  child.on("error", value => { error = value; });
   112	  const closed = new Promise<void>(resolve => child.once("close", () => resolve()));
   113	  return { child, logs, id, closed, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
   114	}
   115	export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
   116	export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
   117	  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
   118	  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
   119	  while (performance.now() < end) {
   120	    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
   121	    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
   122	      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
   123	    }
   124	    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
   125	      const text = logText(path);
   126	      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
   127	      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
   128	        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
   129	        if (url) return url;
   130	      }
   131	      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
   132	    }
   133	    await sleep(25);
   134	  }
   135	  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
   136	}

   375	    pub fn transaction(&mut self) -> Result<Tx<'_>, RuntimeError> {
   376	        self.check_ownership()?;
   377	        // The write lock and identity check are one boundary: an engine
   378	        // opened before another process activated a successor cannot write
   379	        // or read with its stale schema/catalog.
   380	        let tx = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
   381	        if activation::identity(&tx)? != self.identity {
   382	            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the store adopted another backend; reopen this engine".into()));
   383	        }
   384	        Ok(Tx { activation_scan: None, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx, page_size: self.page_size,
   385	            server: self.server,
   386	            catalog: &self.catalog })
   387	    }

   399	    }

   414	    /// An independent read connection, bound to this catalog and identity.
   415	    pub fn reader(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
   416	        self.check_ownership()?;
   417	        let path = self.conn.path().filter(|path| !path.is_empty())
   418	            .ok_or_else(|| {
   419	                RuntimeError::Store("an in-memory store has no independent reader".into())
   420	            })?;
   421	        let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(path, rusqlite::OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY)?;
   422	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — detached readers must answer from the owned database, not a replacement at its path.
   423	        // Compare after opening, before reading. Stat only: closing another raw
   424	        // database descriptor would discard this process's SQLite POSIX locks.
   425	        #[cfg(unix)]
   426	        if file_identity(&conn).ok().flatten() != self.file_identity {
   427	            return Err(RuntimeError::Store(format!("owned database at {path} moved or was replaced; stop and reopen its current path")));
   428	        }
   429	        // This connection skips prepare's schema writes but needs the same
   430	        // planning policy before any statements are cached.
   431	        // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — reuse SQLite plans across bound inputs
   432	        conn.set_db_config(rusqlite::config::DbConfig::SQLITE_DBCONFIG_ENABLE_QPSG, true)?;
   433	        conn.busy_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))?;
   434	        // A reader never owns the directory; only the store that made it removes it.
   435	        let page_size = conn.query_row("PRAGMA page_size", [], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
   436	        Ok(Self { #[cfg(test)] full_adoption: false, adoption_report: Default::default(), conn,
   437	            server: self.server,
   438	            ownership: self.ownership.clone(), #[cfg(unix)] file_identity: self.file_identity, page_size, catalog: self.catalog.clone(), assets_dir: self.assets_dir.clone(), ephemeral_assets: false, identity: self.identity.clone() })
   439	    }

   292	pub(super) fn endpoint(request: Request, shared: &Shared, reset: bool) {
   293	    if reset && !shared.personas {
   294	        return super::respond(request, 403, json!({"denied": snapback4_core::error::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "latency reset at /dev/latency/reset requires dev; run snapback4 dev").site("/dev/latency/reset").rewrite("run snapback4 dev, then snapback4 doctor --latency --reset")}));
   295	    }
   296	    if !shared.personas && let Err(why) = super::service_of(&request, shared) {
   297	        return super::respond(request, 401, json!({"denied": why.site("/dev/latency").rewrite("send Authorization: Bearer <token> from snapback4 token --service <name>")}));
   298	    }
   299	    let store_id = lock(&shared.adoption).store_id.clone();
   300	    // Doctor binds its mutation to the store it inspected, even if the listener
   301	    // port was reused between the status read and this POST.
   302	    if reset && super::query_params(request.url()).get("store_id").is_some_and(|id| id != &store_id) {
   303	        return super::respond(request, 409, json!({"denied": snapback4_core::error::Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the dev server store changed before latency reset").site("/dev/latency/reset").rewrite("retry snapback4 doctor --latency --reset against the current dev server")}));
   304	    }
   305	    let mut snapshot = serde_json::to_value(shared.latency.snapshot(reset)).unwrap();
   306	    snapshot["storeId"] = json!(store_id);
   307	    super::respond(request, 200, snapshot);
   308	}
