# LLP 3000.011.003.000.000: Packaged application proof kit

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Implemented
**Systems:** Snapback 4 proof tooling, starters, fixtures, release source selection
**Author:** Codex for Charlie Cheever
**Date:** 2026-10-14
**Revised:** 2026-10-17 (KIT3 proof and clock contracts); 2026-10-16 (REG missing frontend export diagnostic; §11: wave-14 DAY4); 2026-10-16 (§9: wave-13 DAY3; §10: wave-13 KIT2); 2026-10-16 (§8: explicit platform boundary and client-source guard); 2026-10-15 (§8: wave-12 KIT and observational listener qualification)
**Related:** [Starters](./3000.011.003.000-offline-ready-application-starters.rfc.md), [Release activation](./3000.000.000-retained-storage-and-hot-release.rfc.md), [Built application proof](./3000.011.005.000-qualify-the-built-application.rfc.md)

STAGE staging and persistent startDev defaults are amended by [LLP 3000.000.000.001](./3000.000.000.001-staging-with-real-users.rfc.md); KIT4 owns other ongoing helper changes.

## 1. Contract

Implementer: PROOF workstream, 2026-10-14. Wave 11 fix list 1 authorizes
one published `snapback4/proof` Node module. It owns orchestration, never client
custody or backend/bundler supervision in the product. Existing `startDev(args)`
and `stopChild(child | {url, stop})` recipes remain usable as module exports.

`startDev`, `startServe(directory)`, `startStatic(directory)` and
`startVite(directory)` return `{url, pid, logs, stop}`. Options carry cwd,
environment, arguments and bounded ready/stop deadlines. Read PORT_LO/PORT_HI
at invocation: absent means an OS-assigned port; exactly one set refuses. With
both set, validate 1 <= LO <= HI <= 65535 and bind the first free port in that
inclusive range; exhaustion refuses. Qualification's occupied first port must
be skipped, never adopted. The existing native SNAPBACK4_READY line already names its direct child PID (LLP 3000.011.004.000); no new readiness log is needed. The listening process binds first and keeps that socket;
never adopt an already-serving port. Vite uses its supported JavaScript server
API in a directly spawned Node child, strict ports, the project's config and
a per-launch identity middleware. The directly spawned frontend calls its
server listen API for each candidate; no probe-and-release step exists. With
no range it calls listen on port 0, which the tested Vite API supports with
strictPort; it reads the actual retained socket address. Static hosting uses a directly spawned Node
child with traversal protection, correct wasm MIME and SPA fallback.

All child stdout/stderr go to separate files beneath `logs/`. Read complete
lines from those files, strip ANSI, bound readiness, and reject premature exit.
Native ready lines must name the directly spawned PID. To assert HTTP identity,
the kit sets a random SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID only in the child environment;
`/auth/config` includes `proof_id` only when this variable is nonempty. It
confers no authority or bypass, and ordinary servers omit it. Both static and Vite frontend child
responses carry an `x-snapback4-proof` marker. Identity validation
precedes returning a handle. No public development door is enabled in serve.

Register every spawned child and browser process for an exit-time SIGKILL
sweep, using retained native ChildProcess handles, not process-name kills.
TERM followed by bounded KILL/reap stops a child. Every cleanup action has
its own bound and later actions still run when an earlier action fails.
`cli(args)` runs the native binary, awaits close, returns separate stdout and
stderr, and has a command deadline. It never runs npm/npx or shell strings.

`withBrowser({executablePath: process.env.CHROME}, callback)` launches Chromium
through playwright-core, closes with a bound, and registers its actual browser
process. Root needs --no-sandbox; other hosts first attempt a sandboxed launch
and retry without it only on recognized sandbox-unavailable diagnostics: "No usable sandbox", "Running as
root without --no-sandbox", "Failed to move to new namespace", "SUID sandbox",
"sandbox ... Operation not permitted", or "Failed to unshare ... Operation not
permitted". Unrelated failures never retry.
`waitForFresh` polls for nonempty changed text; `fillRN` clears and types real
keystrokes; `statusText` uses text selectors; `selectByValue` selects stable
option values. The browser executable is CHROME (when provided), undefined executablePath is omitted from launch options, then Playwright's
installed Chromium is used; an absent executable refuses at launch, with its path.
waitForFresh defaults to 30 seconds. Binary resolution is resolveBinary from
the same installed snapback4 package (override SNAPBACK4_BIN only for explicit
workspace tests); the opt-in identity requires CLI/client 0.4.28 or newer and
an older CLI omits proof_id and therefore fails the exact marker assertion; no inferred version comparison or success claim is made. cli(args) does not
invoke package managers; publication uses its existing guarded command runner.

None interprets an HTTP acknowledgement as rendered settlement.

## 2. Explicit source and aliases

`deploy dir --schema file` compiles exactly that saved Quarry file and stages
those same bytes as canonical snapback/schema.q, preserving operation names
regardless of the saved file's name. Compile diagnostics name the selected input.
This happens without swapping the working schema or generating its types.
It does not concatenate other snapback/*.q files. It allocates ordinary numeric
immutable releases, uses the same .pending-adoption marker, transaction/history,
latest selection, restoration-intent preview and no-op checks as normal deploy;
no alternate activation path or restoration bypass exists. The normal deployment path
retains multi-file behavior and activation semantics. Server/native companion source comes from the current project, while all
schema-dependent generated signatures and native builds run against the selected
schema in a private snapshot. A uniquely created snapshot project has
snapback/schema.q, captured companion source, fresh snapback/generated/
and copied native sources (target caches excluded). Host-installed node_modules
is linked into the private project; package.json preserves host module policy.
The snapshot is RAII-owned and removed on success or error; nothing is executed
through the working generated types. Its server entry is typechecked against
the fresh generated server signature before activation; its native crate builds
against fresh generated native.rs into its own native-target directory. Never reuse the working generated signature or
write selected-schema types there. Package prepared signatures and native bytes

    68	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
    69	pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    70	    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
    71	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
    72	            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
    73	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
    74	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    75	    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
    76	        return denied(request);
    77	    };
    78	    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
    79	        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    80	    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
    81	        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
    82	        _ => return denied(request),
    83	    };
    84	    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
    85	        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
    86	        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
    87	            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
    88	        },
    89	        _ => return denied(request),
    90	    };
    91	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    92	    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
    93	        return denied(request);
    94	    }
    95	    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
    96	        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
    97	        _ => return denied(request),
    98	    };
    99	    let binding = mint.binding();
   100	    let Some(server) = server else {
   101	        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
   102	        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
   103	            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
   104	    };
   105	    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
   106	        return denied(request);
   107	    }
   108	    let result = {
   109	        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
   110	        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
   111	        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
   112	        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
   113	            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, snapback4_core::now_ms(), mint.days)
   114	        })
   115	    };
   116	    let reply = match result {
   117	        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
   118	        Err(error) => {
   119	            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
   120	                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
   121	            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
   122	            json!({"denied": why})
   123	        }
   124	    };
   125	    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
   126	        return denied(request);
   127	    };
   128	    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
   129	}
   130	
   131	fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
   132	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
   133	    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
   134	    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
   135	    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
   136	    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
   137	    let mut response = String::new();
   138	    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
   139	    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
   140	        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
   141	        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
   142	        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
   143	}

   298	/// A durable cancellation tombstone closes cancellation versus staged-file commit.
   299	pub fn cancel(request: Request, id: &str, shared: &Shared) {
   300	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
   301	    if id.is_empty() || id.len() > 128 || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_') {
   302	        return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","invalid upload intent id")}));
   303	    }
   304	    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
   305	    let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
   306	    let result = if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id").ok().flatten().as_ref() {
   307	        Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"cancellation belongs to another backend store".into()})
   308	    } else { engine.cancel_asset_intent(&principal,id) };
   309	    drop(engine);
   310	    match result { Ok(()) => respond(request,200,json!({"ok":true})), Err(e) => respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&e.refusal())})) }
   311	}
   312	
   313	/// Finalize only a reported terminal refusal; an existing bound execution always wins.
   314	pub fn finish(request: Request, id: &str, body: Vec<u8>, shared: &Shared) {
   315	    let principal = match principal_of(&request,shared) { Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
   316	    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
   317	    let result = (|| {
   318	        let payload: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&body).map_err(|e| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
   319	        if serde_json::to_vec(&payload).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?.len()>96<<10 {return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_ARGUMENT",message:"finalization envelope exceeds 96 KiB".into()});}
   320	        let name = payload.get("op").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("finalization needs op".into()))?;
   321	        let args = payload.get("args").ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("finalization needs args".into()))?;
   322	        let why: Refusal = serde_json::from_value(payload.get("why").cloned().unwrap_or_default()).map_err(|e| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
   323	        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
   324	        if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"finalization belongs to another backend store".into()}); }
   325	        engine.finish_asset_intent(&principal,id,name,args,&why)
   326	    })();
   327	    match result { Ok(outcome) => respond(request,200,outcome.receipt(Some(id))), Err(error)=>respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})) }
   328	}

   330	/// Owner-only pinned-intent inventory gives storage-loss victims a bounded cleanup path.
   331	pub fn intents(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
   332	    let principal = match principal_of(&request,shared) { Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
   333	    let params=crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
   334	    let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
   335	    let result=(|| {
   336	        if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"intent inventory requires the current backend store_id".into()}); }
   337	        let after=params.get("after").map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
   338	        if after.len()>128 { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("invalid intent continuation".into())); }
   339	        engine.pending_asset_intents(&principal,after)
   340	    })();
   341	    drop(engine);
   342	    match result {Ok(json)=>respond(request,200,json),Err(error)=>respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())}))}
   343	}

   345	/// A bounded declaration binds byte fingerprint and placement before any byte request.
   346	pub fn declare(request:Request,id:&str,body:Vec<u8>,shared:&Shared){
   347	    let principal=match principal_of(&request,shared){Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}))};
   348	    let params=crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
   349	    let result=(||{
   350	        if id.is_empty()||id.len()>128||!id.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()||b==b'-'||b==b'_'){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("invalid upload intent id".into()));}
   351	        let payload:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&body).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
   352	        if serde_json::to_vec(&payload).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?.len()>96<<10 {return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_ARGUMENT",message:"declaration envelope exceeds 96 KiB".into()});}
   353	        let digest=payload["digest"].as_str().filter(|s|s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_digit()||(b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))).ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declaration needs SHA-256 of the selected bytes".into()))?;
   354	        let placement=&payload["placement"];
   355	        let argument=placement["argument"].as_str().filter(|a|!a.is_empty()).ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declaration needs its asset argument".into()))?;
   356	        if placement["op"].as_str().is_none()||!placement["args"].is_object()||placement["args"].get(argument).is_some(){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declare op and placing args omitting the asset argument".into()));}
   357	        let file=payload.get("file").map(|f|snapback4_core::assets::sanitize_name(f["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("")));
   358	        let declared=payload.get("file").and_then(|f|snapback4_core::assets::declared_type(f["type"].as_str()));
   359	        let fingerprint=snapback4_core::value::digest(&(digest,file,declared));
   360	        let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
   361	        if params.get("store_id")!=engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref(){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"declaration belongs to another backend store".into()});}
   362	        engine.declare_asset_intent(&principal,id,&fingerprint,placement)
   363	    })();

    47	/// Bearer sessions are valid on declared public routes even in dev. Personas
    48	/// still pass through the full loopback Host/Origin/peer checks.
    49	fn principal(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
    50	    if !matches!(single_header(request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) { return principal_of(request, shared).map(|c| c.0); }
    51	    let token = crate::auth::bearer_token(request).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
    52	    let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    53	    snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) }).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?
    54	        .map(|(principal, _, _)| principal).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(request, shared)))
    55	}

    57	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — every provider delivery verifies before durable admission or result collection.
    58	pub(super) fn ingress(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, route: RouteSpec) {
    59	    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(route.wait_ms);
    60	    let deadline_ms = shared.now().saturating_add(route.wait_ms as i64);
    61	    let contract = route.contract(name);
    62	    if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = principal(&request, shared) {
    63	        return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
    64	    }
    65	    // A persona is never a provider credential, even on loopback.
    66	    if route.service.is_some() && !matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) {
    67	        return refuse(request, 401, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "a provider route requires its signature, not a development persona");
    68	    }
    69	    let mut envelope = RouteRequest { method: "POST".into(), path: route.path.clone(),
    70	        headers: request.headers().iter().map(|h| (h.field.as_str().to_string(), h.value.as_str().to_owned())).collect(), body_hex: String::new() };
    71	    if envelope.validate(&route).is_err() { return refuse(request, 431, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "route headers exceed their bound or are invalid"); }
    72	    let expected = request.body_length();
    73	    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_BYTES) { return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"); }
    74	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    75	    let mut buffer = [0; 8192];
    76	    loop {
    77	        let width = buffer.len().min(BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() + 1);
    78	        match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..width]) {
    79	            Ok(0) => break,
    80	            Ok(n) if n > BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() => return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"),
    81	            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
    82	            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
    83	            Err(_) => return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body could not be read"),
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n != bytes.len()) { return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body ended before Content-Length"); }
    87	    envelope.body_hex = encode_bytes_hex(&bytes);
    88	    drop(bytes);
    89	    if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms { return unavailable(request); }
    90	    let (mut run, principal) = if route.service.is_some() {
    91	        let authority = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_service_generation(name, &contract)};
    92	        let generation = match authority {
    93	            Ok((_, generation)) => generation, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
    94	        };
    95	        let id = match verification_token() { Ok(id) => id, Err(_) => return unavailable(request) };
    96	        {
    97	            let mut tickets = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets);
    98	            if tickets.len() >= ROUTE_WORKERS { drop(tickets); return unavailable(request); }
    99	            tickets.insert(id.clone(), Ticket { name: name.into(), contract: contract.clone(), generation, envelope,
   100	                deadline, deadline_ms, lease: None, outcome: None });
   101	        }
   102	        let guard = TicketGuard { registry: &shared.route_verification, id };
   103	        shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
   104	        let (id, principal) = loop {
   105	            let outcome = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets).get(&guard.id).and_then(|ticket| ticket.outcome.clone());
   106	            match outcome {
   107	                Some(Verified::Allowed { id, principal }) => break (id, principal),
   108	                Some(Verified::Denied) => return refuse(request, 403, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "the provider signature was refused"),
   109	                Some(Verified::Unavailable) => return unavailable(request),
   110	                None if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => return unavailable(request),
   111	                None => pause(shared, deadline),
   112	            }
   113	        };
   114	        drop(guard);
   115	        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &id, &principal, shared.now())};
   116	        match collected { Ok(run) => (run, principal), Err(_) => return unavailable(request) }
   117	    } else {
   118	        let principal = match principal(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p, Err(why) => return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false) };
   119	        let mut context = Context::new(&principal, BTreeMap::new()).at(shared.now());
   120	        if let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request) { context = context.with_session(token); }
   121	        let admitted = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.request_session_route(name, &contract, &envelope, context)};
   122	        match admitted { Ok((run, _)) => (run, principal), Err(error) => return respond_with_cors(request, if error.refusal().family == "auth" { 401 } else { 503 }, json!({ "denied": error.refusal() }), false) }
   123	    };
   124	    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
   125	    loop {
   126	        if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = self::principal(&request, shared) {
   127	            return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
   128	        }
   129	        // Recheck even an immediately settled replay: revocation and adoption
   130	        // must not turn a retained response into a new authority grant.
   131	        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &run.id, &principal, shared.now())};
   132	        run = match collected {
   133	            Ok(run) => run, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
   134	        };
   135	        if run.state == "done" { return completed(request, &run); }
   136	        if matches!(run.state.as_str(), "failed" | "parked") || Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return unavailable(request); }
   137	        pause(shared, deadline);
   138	    }
   139	}
   140	
   141	fn completed(request: Request, run: &ActionRequest) {
   142	    let response = run.result.clone().and_then(|value| serde_json::from_value::<RouteResponse>(value).ok());
   143	    let Some(response) = response else { return unavailable(request); };
   144	    let Ok(bytes) = response.validate() else { return unavailable(request); };
   145	    if response.status >= 500 { return unavailable(request); }
   146	    let mut output = Response::from_data(bytes).with_status_code(response.status);
   147	    if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){output.add_header(header);}
   148	    for (name, value) in response.headers {
   149	        let Ok(header) = Header::from_bytes(name, value) else { return unavailable(request); };
   150	        output.add_header(header);
   151	    }
   152	    // No JSON re-encoding, credential reflection or implicit CORS: headers
   153	    // and bytes belong to the declared handler, framing to the HTTP server.
   154	    if let Some(header) = super::latency::header() { output.add_header(header); }
   155	    let _ = request.respond_checked_with_completion(output, |result| super::latency::finished(result.is_err()));

     9	fn decode_id(encoded: &str) -> Option<String> {
    10	    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(encoded.len());
    11	    let mut chars = encoded.bytes();
    12	    while let Some(byte) = chars.next() {
    13	        bytes.push(if byte == b'%' {
    14	            let high = (chars.next()? as char).to_digit(16)?;
    15	            let low = (chars.next()? as char).to_digit(16)?;
    16	            (high * 16 + low) as u8
    17	        } else { byte });
    18	    }
    19	    String::from_utf8(bytes).ok()
    20	}

    22	fn outcome(run: &ActionRequest, replayed: bool) -> Json {
    23	    let mut body = match run.state.as_str() {
    24	        "done" => json!({ "state": "done", "id": run.id, "result": run.result }),
    25	        "failed" | "parked" => {
    26	            let code = run.error_code.as_deref().unwrap_or("E_ACTION_FAILED");
    27	            let mapped = snapback4_lang::diag::guide_for(code);
    28	            // Only the closed OIDC settlement set maps to services here.
    29	            // E_AUTH and every non-services mapping retain the legacy action guide.
    30	            let guide = if mapped == "services" && code != "E_AUTH" { mapped } else { "actions" };
    31	            json!({ "state": "failed", "id": run.id, "why": Refusal::new(code, "action", run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or("the handler failed")).guide(guide) })
    32	        }
    33	        _ => {
    34	            let mut why = Refusal::new("E_ACTION_PENDING", "action", "the action is still pending; retry with the same id to collect its result").guide("actions");
    35	            why.retryable = true;
    36	            json!({ "state": "pending", "id": run.id, "why": why })
    37	        }
    38	    };
    39	    if replayed { body["replayed"] = json!(true); }
    40	    body
    41	}

    49	fn arguments(program: &snapback4_core::Program, json: &Json) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    50	    let fields = json.as_object().ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("action args must be an object".into()))?;

    53	        wire::from_json(kind, value).map(|value| (name.clone(), value)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input)

    57	pub(super) fn call(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    58	    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
    59	    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "an action takes { id, args }") })) };
    60	    let Some(id) = payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str) else { return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "mint an id on the client and reuse it when retrying this action") })); };
    61	    let admitted = {
    62	        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
    63	        if let Err(why) = intent_store(&engine, &payload) {
    64	            let metadata = response_head(&mut engine);
    65	            drop(engine);
    66	            return respond(request, 200, match metadata {
    67	                Ok((store_id, head)) => json!({ "denied": why, "store_id": store_id, "head": head }),
    68	                Err(why) => json!({ "denied": why }),
    69	            });
    70	        }
    71	        let raw_args = payload.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| json!({}));
    72	        match engine.replay_action(id, &principal, name, &raw_args, shared.now()) {
    73	            Ok(Some(run)) => Ok((run, true)),
    74	            Err(error) => Err(error),
    75	            Ok(None) => {
    76	                let args = engine.program(name).filter(|p| p.kind == ProgramKind::Action).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_ACTION_UNKNOWN", message: format!("no action {name} is declared") })
    77	                    .and_then(|p| {
    78	                        if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&p.args,&raw_args) {Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(why.message))}
    79	                        else {arguments(p, &raw_args)}
    80	                    });
    81	                args.and_then(|args| {
    82	                    let mut context = Context::new(&principal, args).with_wire_args(raw_args).at(shared.now());
    83	                    if let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request) { context = context.with_session(token); }
    84	                    engine.request_action(name, id, context)
    85	                })
    86	            }
    87	        }
    88	    };
    89	    let (mut run, replayed) = match admitted { Ok(run) => run, Err(error) => { let why = failure(&error); return respond(request, if why.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": why })); } };
    90	    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
    91	    let wait = query_params(request.url()).get("wait").and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(25).min(25);
    92	    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(wait);
    93	    while !matches!(run.state.as_str(), "done" | "failed" | "parked") && Instant::now() < deadline {
    94	        {
    95	            let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
    96	            let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_millis(250))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
    97	        }
    98	        // The caller's session may expire or be revoked while the handler runs.
    99	        if let Err(why) = principal_of(&request, shared) { return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })); }
   100	        run = match producing_engine(shared).action_request_at(id, &principal, shared.now()) { Ok(run) => run, Err(error) => return respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })) };
   101	    }
   102	    if let Err(why) = principal_of(&request, shared) { return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })); }
   103	    respond(request, if matches!(run.state.as_str(), "done" | "failed" | "parked") { 200 } else { 202 }, outcome(&run, replayed));
   104	}

   106	pub(super) fn claim(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {

   110	pub(super) fn claim_set(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {

   114	fn claim_selected(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str, registry: bool) {
   115	    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "claim takes an object") })) };
   116	    let names: Option<Vec<String>> = if registry {
   117	        // The existing whole-request byte bound applies. Do not shorten the
   118	        // accepted operation-name surface: private route names have a prefix.
   119	        let names = payload.get("actions").and_then(Json::as_array).and_then(|values| values.iter().map(|value| value.as_str().filter(|name| !name.is_empty()).map(str::to_owned)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>());
   120	        match names {
   121	            Some(names) if !names.is_empty() && names.len() <= 128 && payload.get("action").is_none() => Some(names),
   122	            _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "claim-set takes actions: 1–128 nonempty names, without action") })),
   123	        }
   124	    } else { None };
   125	    let action = payload.get("action").and_then(Json::as_str);
   126	    let limit = payload.get("limit").and_then(Json::as_u64).unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, 100) as usize;
   127	    let lease = payload.get("lease").and_then(Json::as_i64).unwrap_or(60).clamp(1, 900) * 1_000;
   128	    let session = payload.get("session").and_then(Json::as_i64).unwrap_or(SESSION_SECONDS);
   129	    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(payload.get("wait").and_then(Json::as_u64).unwrap_or(0).min(25));
   130	    loop {
   131	        let (worker, claimed) = {
   132	            let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
   133	            let worker = match service_of_locked(&request, shared, &engine) { Ok(worker) => worker, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
   134	            let claimed = match &names {
   135	                Some(names) => engine.claim_action_set(&worker, names, limit, lease, session, shared.now()),
   136	                None => engine.claim_actions(&worker, action, limit, lease, session, shared.now()),
   137	            };
   138	            (worker, claimed)
   139	        };
   140	        if claimed.as_ref().is_ok_and(|runs| !runs.is_empty()) { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); }
   141	        match claimed {
   142	            Ok(claims) if claims.is_empty() && Instant::now() < deadline => {
   143	                let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
   144	                let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_secs(1))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
   145	            }
   146	            Ok(claims) => return respond(request, 200, json!({ "requests": claims.iter().map(claim_json).collect::<Vec<_>>(), "worker": worker })),
   147	            Err(error) => return respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
   148	        }
   149	    }
   150	}
   151	
   152	pub(super) fn settle(request: Request, shared: &Shared, path: &str, body: &str) {
   153	    if let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/").and_then(|path| path.strip_suffix("/cancel")).and_then(decode_id) {
   154	        let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
   155	        let answer = producing_engine(shared).cancel_action(&id, &principal, shared.now());
   156	        return match answer {
   157	            Ok(run) => { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); respond(request, 200, json!({ "ok": true, "request": outcome(&run, false) })); }
   158	            Err(error) => respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
   159	        };
   160	    }
   161	    let worker = match service_of(&request, shared) { Ok(worker) => worker, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
   162	    let token = match single_header(&request, "x-snapback-action-lease") { Ok(Some(token)) => token.to_owned(), _ => return respond(request, 200, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_ACTION_LEASE", "action", "settle with the claim's session.token in x-snapback-action-lease").guide("actions") })) };
   163	    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "settlement takes { result } or { error, retry }") })) };
   164	    if let Some(tail) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/") {
   165	        if let Some(id) = tail.strip_suffix("/status").and_then(decode_id) {
   166	            let answer = producing_engine(shared).action_status(&id, &worker, &token, shared.now());
   167	            return match answer {
   168	                Ok(run) => respond(request, 200, json!({ "invocation": { "id": run.id, "attempt": run.attempt, "deadline": run.deadline, "cancelled": run.cancelled, "state": run.state, "errorCode": run.error_code } })),
   169	                Err(error) => respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
   170	            };
   171	        }
   172	        if let Some((id, tail)) = tail.split_once("/steps/") {
   173	            if let Some((name, verb)) = tail.rsplit_once('/') {
   174	                if let (Some(id), Some(name)) = (decode_id(id), decode_id(name)) {
   175	                    let field = match verb { "begin" => Some("input"), "done" => Some("result"), _ => None };

   178	/// Routes use the same durable record and authority binding, with their
   179	/// declaration's contract rather than a public action program's contract.
   180	pub(super) fn insert_spec(tx: &Tx<'_>, program: &Program, id: &str, context: &Context, raw: &Json, service: bool, spec: &ActionSpec, contract: &str, now: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   181	    if !spec.work.policy.valid() { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("the action policy is outside its bounds".into())); }
   182	    let authority = context.session_digest.as_deref().map(|digest| identity::authority(tx, digest, &context.viewer, now)).transpose()?;
   183	    let service_generation = if service { Some(identity::principal_generation(tx, &context.viewer)?) } else { None };
   184	    let release: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'release'", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
   185	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _requests(id, name, args, principal, at, state, family, principal_generation, admitted_expires_at, args_digest, spec, contract, deadline, release, service_generation) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, 'queued', ?6, ?7, ?8, ?9, ?10, ?11, ?12, ?13, ?14)",
   186	        params![id, program.name, serde_json::to_string(&context.args).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?, context.viewer, now,
   187	            authority.as_ref().map(|a| &a.family), authority.as_ref().map(|a| a.generation), authority.as_ref().map(|a| a.expires_at), crate::store::payload_digest(raw),
   188	            serde_json::to_string(&spec).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Schema(e.to_string()))?, contract, spec.work.policy.deadline_ms.map(|ms| now.saturating_add(ms as i64)), release, service_generation])?;
   189	    Ok(())
   190	}

   192	impl Engine {
   193	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#9.2 — durable action admission precedes the worker and survives retries by id.
   194	    pub fn request_action(&mut self, name: &str, id: &str, mut context: Context) -> Result<(ActionRequest, bool), RuntimeError> {
   195	        if context.viewer == crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL || !crate::value::identity_fits(&context.viewer) {
   196	            return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("an action needs a signed-in principal".into()));
   197	        }
   198	        if id.len() > 128 || !crate::value::identity_fits(id) || id.starts_with(super::routes::ID_PREFIX) { return Err(RuntimeError::Input("an action id is 1–128 printable characters, minted by the client; route:v1: is reserved for admitted routes".into())); }
   199	        let now = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
   200	        let wire_args = context.wire_args.take().unwrap_or_else(|| Json::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&context.args)));
   201	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   202	        if let Some(recorded) = replay(&tx, id, &context.viewer, name, &wire_args, now)? {
   203	            tx.rollback()?;
   204	            return Ok((recorded, true));
   205	        }
   206	        let program = self.programs.get(name).ok_or_else(unknown)?;
   207	        validate::validate_inputs(&self.schema, program, &mut context.args)?;
   208	        authorize(program, &context.viewer)?;
   209	        let args = serde_json::to_string(&context.args).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
   210	        if args.len() as u64 > self.ceilings.heap_bytes { return Err(RuntimeError::Input("action arguments exceed the heap bound".into())); }
   211	        insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, program, id, &context, &wire_args, false, now)?;
   212	        let run = request(tx.conn(), id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
   213	        tx.commit()?;
   214	        Ok((run, false))
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    pub fn action_request(&self, id: &str, principal: &str) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
   218	        self.action_request_at(id, principal, crate::now_ms())
   219	    }
   220	
   221	    pub fn action_request_at(&self, id: &str, principal: &str, now: i64) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
   222	        let tx = self.store.authority_transaction()?;
   223	        let run = request(tx.conn(), id)?.filter(|run| run.principal == principal).ok_or_else(unknown)?;
   224	        if authority_expiry(&tx, &run, now)?.is_none() { return Err(unknown()); }
   225	        tx.rollback()?;
   226	        Ok(run)
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    pub fn replay_action(&self, id: &str, principal: &str, name: &str, args: &Json, now: i64) -> Result<Option<ActionRequest>, RuntimeError> {
   230	        // The public action fast path must not collect private route responses
   231	        // before ingress has authenticated this delivery (including a replay).
   232	        if id.starts_with(super::routes::ID_PREFIX) || name.starts_with(crate::schema::routes::ACTION_PREFIX) { return Err(unknown()); }
   233	        let tx = self.store.authority_transaction()?;
   234	        let answer = replay(&tx, id, principal, name, args, now)?;
   235	        tx.rollback()?;
   236	        Ok(answer)
   237	    }

   239	    /// `session_seconds` is chosen per action by the worker. A lease ends
   240	    /// at the earlier of its requested duration and the viewer session's.
   241	    pub fn claim_actions(&mut self, worker: &str, action: Option<&str>, limit: usize, lease_ms: i64, session_seconds: i64, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<ActionClaim>, RuntimeError> {
   242	        self.claim_selected_actions(worker, action, None, limit, lease_ms, session_seconds, now)
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    /// A generated registry polls one bounded name set, not one long poll per
   246	    /// handler. Callers group names by their requested session lifetime.
   247	    // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — bounded claim-only coupling cannot starve route verification.
   248	    pub fn claim_action_set(&mut self, worker: &str, actions: &[String], limit: usize, lease_ms: i64, session_seconds: i64, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<ActionClaim>, RuntimeError> {
   249	        if actions.is_empty() || actions.len() > 128 || actions.iter().any(|name| name.is_empty()) {
   250	            return Err(RuntimeError::Input("claim actions must contain 1–128 nonempty names".into()));
   251	        }
   252	        let names = serde_json::to_string(actions).map_err(|error| RuntimeError::Input(error.to_string()))?;
   253	        self.claim_selected_actions(worker, None, Some(&names), limit, lease_ms, session_seconds, now)
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    fn claim_selected_actions(&mut self, worker: &str, action: Option<&str>, names: Option<&str>, limit: usize, lease_ms: i64, session_seconds: i64, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<ActionClaim>, RuntimeError> {
   257	        if !identity::is_service(worker) { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("actions are claimed by a service".into())); }
   258	        let duration = lease_ms.clamp(1_000, MAX_LEASE_MS).min(session_seconds.clamp(1, MAX_LEASE_MS / 1_000) * 1_000);
   259	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   260	        expire(&tx, now)?;
   261	        let ids: Vec<String> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _requests WHERE state = 'queued' AND (next_at IS NULL OR next_at <= ?1) AND (?2 IS NULL OR name = ?2) AND (?4 IS NULL OR name IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?4))) ORDER BY at, id LIMIT ?3")?.query_map(params![now, action, limit.clamp(1, 100) as i64, names], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
   262	        let mut claims = Vec::new();
   263	        for id in ids {
   264	            let run = request(tx.conn(), &id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
   265	            if let Some(contract) = &run.contract {
   266	                let current = if let Some(name) = run.action.strip_prefix(crate::schema::routes::ACTION_PREFIX) {
   267	                    self.schema.routes.get(name).map(|route| route.contract(name))
   268	                } else { self.programs.get(&run.action).map(|program| self.schema.action_specs.get(&run.action).cloned().unwrap_or_default().contract(program)) };
   269	                if current.as_ref() != Some(contract) {
   270	                    finish(&tx, &id, "parked", "E_ACTION_GENERATION", "the admitted action declaration changed; inspect this work before replacing it", now)?;
   271	                    continue;
   272	                }
   273	            }
   274	            // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a claim borrows only authority that still stands.
   275	            let Some(expiry) = authority_expiry(&tx, &run, now)? else {
   276	                tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _requests SET state = 'failed', error_code = 'E_ACTION_AUTHORITY', error = ?2, settled_at = ?3, next_at = NULL, lease_until = NULL, claimed_by = NULL, session_digest = NULL WHERE id = ?1", params![id, AUTHORITY_ERROR, now])?;
   277	                continue;
   278	            };
   279	            let session = identity::action_session(tx.conn(), &run.principal, now, now.saturating_add(duration).min(expiry).min(run.deadline.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)))?;
   280	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _requests SET state = 'running', attempts = attempts + 1, claimed_by = ?2, session_digest = ?3, lease_until = ?4, next_at = NULL, error = NULL, error_code = NULL WHERE id = ?1", params![id, worker, identity::digest(&session.token), session.expires_at])?;

    35	    pub fn request_session_route(&mut self, name: &str, contract: &str, envelope: &RouteRequest, context: Context) -> Result<(ActionRequest, bool), RuntimeError> {
    36	        if declaration(self, name, contract)?.service.is_some() { return Err(unknown()); }
    37	        if context.session_digest.is_none() && !context.viewer.starts_with("dev:") { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("a session route requires a live session".into())); }
    38	        self.admit_route(name, contract, envelope, context, None)

    41	    /// The trusted ingress host calls this only after its fenced verifier
    42	    /// answered allow. No public action endpoint calls this method.
    43	    // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — route/body deduplication starts after provider authentication, before handler execution.
    44	    pub fn request_verified_route(&mut self, name: &str, contract: &str, envelope: &RouteRequest, service_generation: i64, now: i64) -> Result<(ActionRequest, bool), RuntimeError> {
    45	        let principal = declaration(self, name, contract)?.service.clone().ok_or_else(unknown)?;
    46	        self.admit_route(name, contract, envelope, Context::new(principal, BTreeMap::new()).at(now), Some(service_generation))
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    fn admit_route(&mut self, name: &str, contract: &str, envelope: &RouteRequest, mut context: Context, service_generation: Option<i64>) -> Result<(ActionRequest, bool), RuntimeError> {
    50	        let route = declaration(self, name, contract)?.clone();
    51	        let body = envelope.validate(&route).map_err(|message| refused("E_ROUTE_INPUT", message))?;
    52	        if context.viewer == crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL || !crate::value::identity_fits(&context.viewer) { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("a route needs a signed-in principal or its declared service seat".into())); }
    53	        let now = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
    54	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
    55	        let scope = if let Some(expected) = service_generation {
    56	            if route.service.as_deref() != Some(context.viewer.as_str()) || identity::principal_generation(&tx, &context.viewer)? != expected {
    57	                return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("the service authority changed during route verification".into()));
    58	            }
    59	            // Do not re-key service work after a declaration/seat change.
    60	            // Existing same-body work must refuse, not execute a second time.
    61	            json!(["service"])
    62	        } else if let Some(token) = &context.session_digest {
    63	            let authority = identity::authority(&tx, token, &context.viewer, now)?;
    64	            json!(["session", context.viewer, authority.family, authority.generation])
    65	        } else { json!(["dev", context.viewer]) };
    66	        let body_digest = format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&body));
    67	        let id = format!("{ID_PREFIX}{}", crate::value::digest(&(name, scope, body_digest)));
    68	        let program = route.program(name);
    69	        if let Some(run) = actions::request(tx.conn(), &id)? {
    70	            if run.action != program.name || run.contract.as_deref() != Some(contract) { return Err(generation()); }
    71	            if run.principal != context.viewer || actions::authority_expiry(&tx, &run, now)?.is_none() { return Err(unknown()); }
    72	            // Headers (e.g. fresh signatures/timestamps) are authentication
    73	            // metadata, not a second intent. Retain the first admitted envelope.
    74	            let previous = run.args.get("request").map(wire::to_json).ok_or_else(unknown)?;
    75	            if previous.get("bodyHex").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) != Some(envelope.body_hex.as_str()) { return Err(crate::store::id_reused()); }
    76	            tx.rollback()?;
    77	            return Ok((run, true));
    78	        }
    79	        // Verification sees incoming credentials, but durable handlers receive
    80	        // bound authority, not a reusable bearer/cookie copied into work storage.
    81	        let mut retained = envelope.clone();
    82	        retained.headers.retain(|(name, _)| !matches!(name.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(), "authorization" | "proxy-authorization" | "cookie" | "x-snapback-persona"));
    83	        let request = serde_json::to_value(retained).map_err(|_| refused("E_ROUTE_INPUT", "cannot encode route envelope"))?;
    84	        context.args = BTreeMap::from([("request".into(), wire::untyped(&request).map_err(|message| refused("E_ROUTE_INPUT", message))?)]);
    85	        let raw = json!({"request": request});
    86	        if serde_json::to_vec(&context.args).map_err(|error| RuntimeError::Input(error.to_string()))?.len() as u64 > self.ceilings.heap_bytes {
    87	            return Err(refused("E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route envelope exceeds the engine heap bound"));
    88	        }
    89	        actions::insert_spec(&tx, &program, &id, &context, &raw, service_generation.is_some(), &route.action_spec(), contract, now)?;
    90	        let run = actions::request(tx.conn(), &id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
    91	        tx.commit()?;
    92	        Ok((run, false))
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    /// Collection checks both the currently declared route and the original
    96	    /// authority. The HTTP host also checks its current caller/verified ticket.
    97	    pub fn route_request_at(&self, name: &str, contract: &str, id: &str, principal: &str, now: i64) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
    98	        declaration(self, name, contract)?;
    99	        let run = self.action_request_at(id, principal, now)?;
   100	        if run.action != format!("{ACTION_PREFIX}{name}") || !id.starts_with(ID_PREFIX) { return Err(unknown()); }
   101	        if run.contract.as_deref() != Some(contract) { return Err(generation()); }
   102	        Ok(run)
   103	    }
   104	}

   178	    /// Bounded owner-only recovery of pinned uploads after device storage loss.
   179	    pub fn pending_asset_intents(&mut self, owner: &str, after: &str) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
   180	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   181	        let entries: Vec<(String,String)> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT u.intent,u.facts FROM _asset_intents u JOIN _assets a ON a.id=u.asset WHERE u.owner=?1 AND u.intent>?2 AND u.state='pending' AND a.home_table IS NULL AND a.released_at IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _ops o WHERE o.principal=u.owner AND o.id=u.intent) ORDER BY u.intent LIMIT 51")?.query_map([owner,after], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   182	        let next = if entries.len()>50 { Some(entries[49].0.clone()) } else { None };
   183	        let mut intents=Vec::new();
   184	        for (id,facts) in entries.into_iter().take(50) {
   185	            let asset: Asset = crate::json_codec::from_text(&facts).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   186	            let mut facts=serde_json::json!({"id":asset.id,"type":asset.content_type,"bytes":asset.bytes,"width":asset.width,"height":asset.height,"duration":asset.duration});
   187	            if let Some(name)=asset.name {facts["name"]=serde_json::json!(name);}
   188	            intents.push(serde_json::json!({"id":id,"asset":facts,"createdAt":asset.created_at}));
   189	        }
   190	        let store = tx.meta("store_id")?;
   191	        tx.rollback()?;
   192	        Ok(serde_json::json!({"intents":intents,"next":next,"store_id":store}))
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    /// Persist early terminal input/operation refusals for an owned upload placement.
   196	    /// This closes paths that return before ordinary mutation receipt creation.
   197	    pub fn finish_asset_intent(&mut self, owner: &str, id: &str, name: &str, args: &serde_json::Value, why: &Refusal) -> Result<MutationOutcome, RuntimeError> {
   198	        if why.retryable { return Err(RuntimeError::Input("retryable outcomes cannot finalize an upload intent".into())); }
   199	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   200	        if !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _asset_intents WHERE owner=?1 AND intent=?2)", [owner,id], |r| r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   201	            return Err(RuntimeError::Asset {code:"E_UPLOAD_INTENT",message:"this viewer has no uploaded intent under that id".into()});
   202	        }
   203	        if let Some(receipt) = crate::store::replay_bound(tx.conn(), owner, id, name, args)? {
   204	            let outcome = MutationOutcome::from_receipt(&receipt)?; tx.rollback()?; return Ok(outcome);
   205	        }
   206	        let cancelled=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT state='cancelled' FROM _asset_intents WHERE owner=?1 AND intent=?2",[owner,id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?;
   207	        if cancelled {tx.rollback()?;return Ok(MutationOutcome{receipt_only:true,state:WriteState::Failed{why:Refusal::new("E_UPLOAD_CANCELLED","asset","this intent was durably cancelled"),current:None},metered:Metered::default(),tables:Vec::new(),events:0,replayed:true});}
   208	        assets::require_placement(&tx,owner,id,name,args)?;
   209	        if why.code.chars().count()>128 || why.family.chars().count()>128 || why.message.chars().count()>4096 || why.guide.as_ref().is_some_and(|g|g.chars().count()>128) {
   210	            return Err(RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_ARGUMENT",message:"finalization refusal exceeds its 128-character fields or 4096-character message allowance".into()});
   211	        }
   212	        let outcome = MutationOutcome { receipt_only:false, state:WriteState::Failed {why:why.clone(),current:None}, metered:Metered::default(),tables:Vec::new(),events:0,replayed:false };
   213	        let seq = tx.next_seq()? - 1;
   214	        tx.record_applied(owner,id,seq,&crate::json_codec::to_text(&outcome.receipt(Some(id))).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
   215	        tx.bind_applied(owner,id,name,args,crate::now_ms())?;
   216	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _asset_intents SET state='settled' WHERE owner=?1 AND intent=?2 AND state != 'cancelled'", [owner,id])?;
   217	        tx.commit()?; Ok(outcome)
   218	    }
   219	
   220	    /// Cancellation fences a late staged upload too; executed placements cannot be cancelled.
   221	    pub fn cancel_asset_intent(&mut self, owner: &str, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   222	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   223	        if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _ops WHERE principal=?1 AND id=?2)", [owner,id], |r| r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   224	            return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_UPLOAD_DISPATCHED", message: "placement already has a server outcome; recover its receipt".into() });
   225	        }
   226	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _asset_intents(owner,intent,state) VALUES(?1,?2,'cancelled') ON CONFLICT(owner,intent) DO UPDATE SET state='cancelled'", [owner,id])?;
   227	        tx.commit()
   228	    }

codex
The auth sweep found another gap: queued persona requests can still reach action admission, session-route admission, and asset-intent operations after password-only adoption closes the doors.

The deadlock is limited to the true in-memory fallback used by Rust test hosts. Native `--memory` uses a disposable WAL file, so that path is unaffected.
codex
**NOT READY** — reviewed committed candidate `f5c19ffbed8203351bc0bc6525abd918a3c0298e` against the sb4-loop merge base `ccb9f752d198adb392e0b9def727de3a0ee17450`.

1. **[P1] Persona authority can survive password-only adoption on omitted execution paths.**

   [actions.rs:58](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/actions.rs:58) authenticates the persona before acquiring the writer at line 62, then calls `request_action` without `mutation_authority`. A request can authenticate while doors are open, queue behind adoption, and create durable action work under the replacement password-only schema. Persona requests supply no session digest, so the engine’s session validation does not close this gap. The later HTTP recheck can return 401 **after the work has already been admitted**.

   The same gap exists in session-route admission at [serve_routes.rs:118](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_routes.rs:118): authentication precedes writer acquisition, and `request_session_route` explicitly accepts a `dev:` principal without a session.

   Asset-intent operations also authenticate before writer acquisition without revalidation: cancellation at [assets.rs:300](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/assets.rs:300), finalization at line 315, inventory at line 332, and declaration at line 347. For example, queued cancellation can commit a cancellation tombstone after the persona door has closed.

   These are implementation gaps in the amendment’s adoption boundary. The new checks cover ordinary mutations, queries, sync, asset downloads and selected system doors, but do not cover all uses of persona authority.

   **Repair:** revalidate against the exact engine used for admission, mutation, replay or inventory, while holding its guard. Apply the same rule to the upload publication path. Add deterministic queued-adoption tests for actions, session routes and asset intents, asserting absence of durable effects as well as refusal responses.

2. **[P2] The new persona guard deadlocks accepted queries on true in-memory stores.**

   [serve.rs:1288](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve.rs:1288) retains the guard returned by `persona_read_authority`, which locks `shared.bound` at [serve_identity.rs:95](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_identity.rs:95). Before releasing it, [serve.rs:1297](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve.rs:1297) locks that same non-reentrant mutex again to obtain refusal sites.

   This blocks accepted persona queries in the Rust in-memory HTTP fixtures. Existing tests already exercise it—for example, `dev_loopback_admits_eight_simultaneous_sign_ins_within_host_capacity` queries `/q/who` at [serve_tests.rs:1113](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_tests.rs:1113). Native `--memory` uses a disposable WAL file, so this finding concerns the true in-memory fallback and its test coverage.

   **Repair:** obtain refusal sites from the retained `Bound` guard, avoiding recursive acquisition while preserving the guard through evaluation.

The store-bound staging classification, production bulk-seed refusal, private operator-token capture, and first-admin guide’s independent operator-only roster rules showed no additional material defect in this inspection.

This was read-only source review. No Cargo, full suite or long qualification was run. Platform and packed qualification remain separate evidence; the findings above are implementation defects, not missing future qualification.
